### [1] [Bounded curvature manifolds without Euclidean isometric immersions of bounded mean curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19168)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文构造了 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上一类完备光滑黎曼度量，曲率范数与单射半径均有界，但不存在具有有界平均曲率的 $C^2$ 等距浸入到有限维欧氏空间，从而在更强曲率假设下否定了 Yau 问题 52。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 问题 52 询问是否存在具有有界几何（如曲率与单射半径受控）的完备黎曼流形，不能等距浸入到欧氏空间中且保持平均曲率有界。此前结果多在较弱假设下讨论；本文希望在“全曲率有界”这一更强假设下给出反例，明确问题的否定答案。

Method: 对每个 $n\ge2$ 在 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上构造光滑完备度量 $G_n$，使全曲率范数至多为 1、单射半径至少为 1。二维情形利用共形因子拉普拉斯的有限 Jacobian 表示：若第二基本形式有界，则对应表示受一致控制；随后构造互不相交的共形块，使曲率保持有界而有限 Jacobian 表示代价趋于无穷。高维通过取欧氏乘积推广。

Result: 构造出的度量 $G_n$ 满足全曲率范数有界与单射半径有界，但任何到有限维欧氏空间的 $C^2$ 等距浸入都不具有有界平均曲率；二维中表现为第二基本形式无界，高维由乘积保持该性质。

Conclusion: 在比原问题更强的“全曲率有界”假设下，仍存在不能以有界平均曲率等距浸入欧氏空间的完备流形，因此 Yau 问题 52 的答案为否定。

Abstract: For every integer $n\ge2$, we construct a smooth complete Riemannian metric $G_n$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with full curvature norm at most one and injectivity radius at least one for which no $C^2$ isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space has bounded mean curvature. In dimension two, bounded second fundamental form would give uniformly controlled finite Jacobian representations of the Laplacian of the conformal factor. We construct disjoint conformal blocks for which the curvature remains bounded while the finite Jacobian representation cost tends to infinity. Taking Euclidean products gives all higher dimensions. The result answers Yau's Problem~52 negatively under the stronger assumption of bounded full curvature.

</details>


### [2] [Umbilic slopes and cubic Weingarten surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19188)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究脐点斜率与三次Weingarten曲面的全局分类：非定常主曲率芽的唯一割线切线斜率受限，非定常脐点芽处平均曲率与高斯曲率的正则有关系提供此类切线；并分类满足 κ₂=cκ₁³ 的紧致无边界浸入曲面为旋转椭球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨脐点处主曲率的结构以及三次Weingarten曲面（主曲率满足三次关系）的全局几何性质，寻求对此类曲面形状的完整分类。

Method: 利用脐点处齐次Hessian恒等式的分类；以标量唯一延拓处理光滑斜率问题中的无穷阶接触；通过精确的四阶增长估计与角度能量估计完成三次曲面分类。

Result: 非定常主曲率芽若具有唯一割线切线，其斜率为零、负一、无穷、不小于三的奇数或其倒数；非定常脐点芽处，有序曲率像至多有两个极限方向且均属同一集合；满足 κ₂=cκ₁³（某顺序，c 固定实数）的 C⁴ 紧致无边界浸入曲面，其每个连通分支嵌入为旋转椭球面。

Conclusion: 刻画了脐点处主曲率切线的可能斜率集合，并证明三次Weingarten关系下紧致浸入曲面只能是旋转椭球面，统一方法在于脐点处齐次Hessian恒等式的分类。

Abstract: We study umbilic slopes and the global classification of cubic Weingarten surfaces. For a smooth surface in Euclidean three-space, a nonconstant principal curvature germ with a unique secant tangent has slope zero, minus one, infinity, an odd integer at least three, or its reciprocal. At a nonconstant umbilic germ, a smooth regular relation between mean and Gaussian curvature supplies such a tangent after a continuous labeling of the principal curvatures; the ordered curvature image has at most two limiting directions, both in the same set. We also classify compact immersed surfaces without boundary of class $C^4$ satisfying $κ_2=cκ_1^3$ in some order at every point, for a fixed real $c$: each connected component is embedded as an ellipsoid of revolution. The arguments share a classification of homogeneous Hessian identities at an umbilic. Scalar unique continuation treats infinite-order contact in the smooth slope problem, whereas an exact fourth-order growth estimate and angular energy estimates yield the cubic classification.

</details>


### [3] [Mean curvature flows with cylindrical singularities II: stability and genericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19390)
*Ao Sun,Jinxin Xue*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究柱状奇点（cylindrical singularity）的非退化性，证明非退化柱状奇点在微小扰动下具有稳定性，并进一步证明可通过扰动带有退化奇点的平均曲率流，使其产生非退化奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前在[SX25]中借助动力系统思想引入了非退化柱状奇点的概念，本文旨在深入刻画这类奇点的性质，特别是其稳定性与可构造性，从而加深对平均曲率流奇点结构的理解。

Method: 沿用动力系统视角分析柱状奇点的非退化条件，通过扰动分析证明其稳定性；并构造对具有退化奇点的平均曲率流的适当扰动，使其奇点变为非退化。

Result: 非退化柱状奇点在小扰动下保持稳定；存在扰动使得原本具有退化奇点的平均曲率流产生非退化奇点。

Conclusion: 非退化性是一个稳定且可通过扰动实现的奇点性质，这为平均曲率流奇点分析提供了更灵活的工具。

Abstract: In [SX25], we introduced the notion of nondegenerate cylindrical singularity using the ideas from dynamical systems. In this paper, we further study the properties of these singularities, showing that they are stable under small perturbations. Furthermore, we show that we can perturb some mean curvature flow with degenerate singularities to produce nondegenerate singularities.

</details>


### [4] [Complete Self-Shrinkers in Arbitrary Codimension with Positive Constant Scalar Curvature and \(S\le 1\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19464)
*Shunzi Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究任意余维数p≥1的完备自收缩子，在标量曲率为正常数且第二基本形式模长平方S≤1的条件下，证明S≡1且流形等距于球面或广义柱面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自收缩子是平均曲率流的自相似解，在奇点分析中具有核心地位。已有结果多集中在余维数1（超曲面）情形，高余维数的刚性分类结果较少，作者希望确定在标量曲率为正常数、第二基本形式模长有界的强假设下，高余维数是否存在新的例子。

Method: 通过一个代数引理给出Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率的统一正下界；结合Wei–Wylie比较定理、Cheng–Zhou关于有限高斯体积与多项式体积增长等价性的结论，以及Cao–Li的间隙定理完成证明。

Result: 证明了S≡1，并且X等距于圆球S^n(√n)或标准广义柱面S^k(√k)×R^{n-k}（2≤k≤n−1）。特别地，在这些假设下不会出现新的高余维数例子。

Conclusion: 在高余维数情形下，标量曲率为正常数与S≤1的假设足以强制自收缩子退化为经典的球面或广义柱面，表明刚性现象不依赖于余维数为1的限制。

Abstract: Let \(X:M^n\to\mathbb R^{n+p}\) be an \(n\)-dimensional complete self-shrinker in arbitrary codimension \(p\ge 1\). Suppose that the scalar curvature \(R\) is a positive constant and that the squared norm \(S\) of the second fundamental form satisfies \(S\le 1\). We prove that \(S\equiv 1\) and that \(X\) is isometric to either the round sphere \(S^n(\sqrt n)\) or the standard generalized cylinder \(S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}\), \(2\le k\le n-1\). In particular, no new higher-codimension examples occur under these assumptions. The proof uses an algebraic lemma which yields a uniform positive lower bound for the Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature, together with the comparison theorem of Wei--Wylie, the equivalence of finite Gaussian volume and polynomial volume growth due to Cheng--Zhou, and the gap theorem of Cao--Li.

</details>


### [5] [Infinitesimmaly homogeneous manifolds with symplectic and almost contact structure groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19568)
*Carlos Alberto Marín Arango,Alejandro Pinilla Barrera*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究指定结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射流形：对Sp(2n,R)证明对应结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；对U(n)×{1}（等价于殆切触度量数据）分类无挠特征张量，并通过不变形变给出一般挠率情形，得到相容联络与trans-Sasakian联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画无穷小齐性仿射流形在给定结构群下的具体几何实现，将抽象的G-结构/齐性条件与经典几何结构（辛仿射、殆切触度量、trans-Sasakian）建立对应，从而对允许的挠率与内挠率进行分类与约束。

Method: 研究无穷小齐性仿射流形及指定结构群。对Sp(2n,R)直接证明结构刻画；对U(n)×{1}，先分类无挠特征张量，再用不变形变构造一般挠率情形，并分析可容许挠率与内挠率参数满足的代数关系、水平分布的切触性判据及相容联络。

Result: Sp(2n,R)对应的无穷小齐性结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构。对U(n)×{1}，完成无挠特征张量分类，得到一般挠率情形；可容许挠率与内挠率参数受单一代数关系支配，水平分布在参数某组合非零时恰为切触的，并给出了相容联络及无挠分支与trans-Sasakian几何的联系。

Conclusion: 无穷小齐性框架能统一刻画辛仿射与殆切触度量两类结构：前者刚性为平坦无挠辛仿射；后者的挠率允许族由单一代数关系控制，切触性由参数组合判定，且无挠分支对应trans-Sasakian几何。

Abstract: We study infinitesimally homogeneous affine manifolds with prescribed structure groups. First, for the real symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}(2n,\mathbb R)$, we prove that the corresponding infinitesimally homogeneous structures are precisely flat torsion-free symplectic affine structures. Second, for \(\operatorname{U}(n)\times\{1\}\), whose \(G\)-structures are equivalent to almost contact metric data, we classify the torsion-free characteristic tensors and obtain the general torsional case by invariant deformation. The admissible torsion and inner-torsion parameters are governed by a single algebraic relation, and the horizontal distribution is contact precisely when a certain combination of these parameters does not vanish. We also describe the compatible connections and relate the torsion-free branches to trans-Sasakian geometry.

</details>


### [6] [Spectral Geometry of Hurwitz Spaces with Arbitrary Ramification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19604)
*Jia-Ming,Liou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究由紧黎曼面上非恒定亚纯函数拉回的球面度量的Friedrichs Laplacian，对任意分歧剖面（包括同一分支值上的多个分歧点）证明了zeta正则化行列式的局部公式，其证明结合光滑平凡化、resolvent幂的迹范数变分、球面锥模型的矩阵比较以及Davies--Gaffney估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画由亚纯函数拉回球面度量所诱导的Laplacian的zeta正则化行列式，并给出对任意分歧剖面（允许同一分支值对应多个分歧点）都成立的局部显式公式。现有结果通常对分歧结构有额外限制，该文旨在去除这些限制并保持常数与Hurwitz坐标无关。

Method: 证明综合使用了光滑平凡化与resolvent幂的trace-norm变分，并将算子与球面锥模型进行矩阵比较。利用Davies--Gaffney估计获得所需的高能一致控制，而零能项则通过Schiffer双微分与Rauch变分公式来识别。

Result: 对任意分歧剖面证明了行列式局部公式 Det_ζ(Δ_{[φ],F})=C·det Im B·|τ_B|^2·∏_{k=1}^{N}ρ(z_k,\bar z_k)^{c_k}（省略零特征值）。其中B为周期矩阵，τ_B为局部Bergman tau函数，z_k为分支值坐标，ρ(z,\bar z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}，c_k=(1/12)∑_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})由第k个分支值上的分歧指数n_{kj}给出；常数C>0与Hurwitz坐标无关，亏格零时取det Im B=1。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了Friedrichs Laplacian zeta行列式在任意分歧剖面下的局部分解公式，并将整体量与局部几何因子分离，常数不依赖于Hurwitz坐标。结果覆盖亏格零情形，并将高能与零能贡献分别通过统一估计与变分公式处理。

Abstract: We study the Friedrichs Laplacian associated with the pullback of the round metric on \(\mb P^1\) by a nonconstant meromorphic function \(\varphi:X\to\mb P^1\) on a compact Riemann surface. For arbitrary ramification profiles, including several ramification points over the same branch value, we prove the local formula \[ \operatorname{Det}_ζ(Δ_{[\varphi],\mc F}) =C\,\det\operatorname{Im}B\,|τ_B|^2 \prod_{k=1}^Nρ(z_k,\overline{z_k})^{c_k}. \] The zero eigenvalue is omitted. Here \(B\) is the period matrix, \(τ_B\) is the local Bergman tau-function, \(z_k\) are the branch-value coordinates, \(ρ(z,\overline z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}\), and \(c_k=\frac1{12}\sum_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})\), where \(n_{kj}\) are the ramification indices over the \(k\)-th branch value. The constant \(C>0\) is independent of the Hurwitz coordinates, and \(\det\operatorname{Im}B\) is taken to be \(1\) in genus zero. The proof combines smooth trivializations and trace-norm variation of resolvent powers with matrix comparison to spherical conic models. The Davies--Gaffney estimate provides the required uniform high-energy control, while the zero-energy terms are identified through the Schiffer bidifferential and Rauch variational formulas.

</details>


### [7] [A stable self-shrinking Möbius bundle in $\mathbb R^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19648)
*Kahnrad Braxton,Tang-Kai Lee,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了R^4中拓扑为Möbius带的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子的线性稳定性，给出高余维中非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与稳定超曲面收缩子的分类结论形成鲜明对比，探索高余维稳定收缩子是否存在非平凡类型，并理解其拓扑意义。

Method: 对R^4中具有Möbius带拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子进行线性稳定性分析（证明）。

Result: 该Möbius收缩子是线性稳定的，从而在高余维中给出了非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子。

Conclusion: Möbius收缩子拓扑上对应实blow-up的逆转，暗示稳定的高余维奇点可能编码平均曲率流下的非平凡拓扑操作。

Abstract: We prove the linear stability of an embedded mean curvature flow shrinker in $\mathbb R^4$ with the topology of the Möbius bundle. This provides a non-flat, non-spherical stable shrinker in higher codimension, in sharp contrast with the classification of stable hypersurface shrinkers. Topologically, the Möbius shrinker models the reversal of a real blow-up, suggesting that stable higher-codimension singularities may encode non-trivial topological operations under mean curvature flow.

</details>


### [8] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一类单端渐近平坦、带多时间方向与光滑紧致边界的自旋初值数据的正质量定理，在迹范数支配能量条件与第二基本形式两两对易假设下，得到边界不等式 E ≥ ‖P‖_tr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典正质量定理推广到含边界、多时间方向的自旋初值数据情形，并厘清边界项对能量下界的影响，给出带混合边界项的精确不等式与最优性刻画。

Method: 在仿射能量空间中构造 Dirac–Witten 调和旋量，通过逼近方法计算质量恒等式；利用每个边界分支上固定辅助时间方向确定的局部手性条件，识别存留的混合边界项，并分析给定投影子下边界条件 H + tr_Σ k^T + ‖Q_T‖_tr ≤ 0 的点态最优性。

Result: 在迹范数支配能量条件与第二基本形式两两对易下，若边界满足 H + tr_Σ k^T + ‖Q_T‖_tr ≤ 0，则能量满足 E ≥ ‖P‖_tr；并证明该边界条件对固定投影子是点态最优的，同时给出具有非零混合边界项的双时间数据例子。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了带边界与多时间方向自旋初值数据的正质量定理，明确了边界手性条件与混合边界项的作用，并获得能量与动量迹范数之间的尖锐下界，说明所得边界条件不可进一步改进。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>


### [9] [Deforming area-preserving maps between surfaces by mean curvature flow coupled with Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19724)
*Ping-Hung Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 通过标准化Ricci流演化两个紧Riemann曲面上的度量、用平均曲率流演化保面积映射的图像，证明该流长期存在并指数收敛到极小Lagrangian图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Wang与Smoczyk关于常标量曲率Riemann曲面间保面积映射形变的结果推广到一般度量情形，即允许曲面度量为非常曲率。

Method: 对两个紧Riemann曲面上的度量分别施加标准化Ricci流，同时对保面积映射的图像施加平均曲率流，形成耦合流。

Result: 证明该耦合流对所有时间存在，始终保持为保面积映射的图像，并光滑且指数收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小Lagrangian图。

Conclusion: 该工作将Wang和Smoczyk在常标量曲率假设下的结论推广至任意紧Riemann曲面情形，确立了此类耦合几何流的长时存在性与指数收敛性。

Abstract: We study a natural way to deform area-preserving maps between compact Riemann surfaces. Specifically, we evolve the metrics on the two Riemann surfaces by the normalized Ricci flow and the graph of the area-preserving map by the mean curvature flow. We prove that the flow exists for all time, remains the graph of an area-preserving map, and converges smoothly and exponentially to a minimal Lagrangian graph with respect to the product of the limiting metrics. This generalizes earlier results of Wang and Smoczyk, in which the Riemann surfaces have constant scalar curvature.

</details>


### [10] [Total scalar curvature under a curvature operator lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19851)
*Jian Ge,Chuanhuan Li,Ronggang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 在曲率算子不小于单位球面的完备单连通Riemann流形上，证明标量曲率总体积积分不超过单位球面的相应值，等号成立当且仅当等距于单位圆球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究曲率算子有下界的流形上标量曲率的整体积分上界，即Bishop-Gromov型或刚性型积分估计，并推广到含体积项的更强不等式。

Method: 偶数维情形利用Chern-Gauss-Bonnet公式；奇数维情形应用相应边界公式于Deruelle的Ricci expander filling。

Result: 证明∫_M scal dVol ≤ n(n-1)ω_n，等号成立当且仅当(M,g)等距于单位圆球；并得到含Vol(M,g)的更强界。

Conclusion: 在曲率算子不小于单位球面的条件下，标量曲率积分受单位球面值控制，且刚性刻画单位球，偶数维与奇数维分别通过Chern-Gauss-Bonnet公式及Ricci expander filling实现。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n}, g)$ be a complete, simply connected Riemannian manifold without boundary, of dimension $n\ge3$, with curvature operator at least that of the unit sphere. We prove that $$\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\ d {\rm Vol}_x\le n(n-1)ω_n,$$ where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit $n$-sphere. Equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the unit round sphere. In fact, we obtain a stronger bound containing ${\rm Vol}(M, g)$. In even dimensions, the proof follows from the Chern-Gauss-Bonnet formula. In odd dimensions, we apply the corresponding boundary formula to Deruelle's Ricci expander filling.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we prove all gaps in the Simon conjecture for connected closed minimal surfaces immersed in unit spheres. Our proof uses the line-bundle ladder of Lin, Wang, and Xu and a Dolbeault computation to obtain two-sided ordered spectral comparisons on the two-sphere under Gaussian curvature $K_g\geq1$ and $K_g\leq1$, with rigidity in the equality case. Applying these comparisons at consecutive Calabi scales forces the Gaussian curvature to be one of the two endpoint values. We also establish the corresponding rigidity theorem for closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector. Finally, we prove a rotationally symmetric higher-dimensional analogue under a Ricci lower bound and a sectional-curvature upper bound.

</details>


### [14] [Biharmonic Conformal Surfaces and Dirac Factorization I: Exact Spinorial Encoding and Scalar--Chiral Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20197)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究3维空间形式中非极小双调和浸入的Ou方程组，将其重构为单一Dirac型方程，并在单值标量-手性类中对算子分解进行完全分类，证明其刚性过强，无法产生新的非CMC例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双调和性是调和映射的四阶推广，但不具共形不变性，因此非极小浸入可通过改变区域度量变为双调和。Ou在3维空间形式中将该问题约化为关于加权平均曲率U=λ²H的两个耦合方程；作者希望把这两个方程组织成单一Dirac型方程，并考察与一阶分解相容的几何条件。

Method: 将环境Killing旋量限制到曲面，构造自然的Laplace型算子ℬ_c，证明ℬ_c(Uψ)=0恰好等价于Ou方程组。随后在单值标量–手性类(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)中对ℬ_c的所有分解进行分类，利用Dirac判别式9c−4|A|²分析刚性机制，并通过模型例子对比几何算子分解与正共形模的存在性。

Result: 在非零曲率情形，每个此类分解自动为平均曲率归一化，且局部存在当且仅当曲面具有局部常主曲率；归一化欧氏分支同样刚性，其余欧氏因子构成例外全纯–反全纯族，在非平面点处由log(|A|²−H²)的调和性刻画。假设的非CMC实分支归结为球面Gauss–Codazzi系统，其精确Frobenius挠率严格为负。模型例子表明几何算子的分解与正共形模的存在性不同。

Conclusion: 标量–手性通道虽完备但刚性过强，无法生成新的非CMC例子，为更广泛的Clifford值构造提供了精确基线。

Abstract: For maps from surfaces, harmonicity is conformally invariant and a conformal immersion is harmonic precisely when its image is minimal. Biharmonicity is a fourth-order extension of this theory, but it is not conformally invariant. A nonminimal immersion may therefore become biharmonic after a suitable change of the domain metric. In a three-dimensional space form, Ou's formulation reduces this problem to two coupled equations for the weighted mean curvature $U=λ^2H$: one scalar equation and one tangential equation.
  We ask whether these two equations can be organized as a single Dirac-type equation and what geometry is compatible with a first-order factorization. Restricting an ambient Killing spinor to the surface, we construct a natural Laplace-type operator $\mathscr B_c$ and prove that $\mathscr B_c(Uψ)=0$ is exactly equivalent to Ou's system. We then classify every factorization of $\mathscr B_c$ in the monic scalar--chiral class $(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)$. In nonzero curvature, every such factorization is automatically mean-curvature-normalized and exists locally if and only if the surface has locally constant principal curvatures. The same rigidity holds for the normalized Euclidean branch; the remaining Euclidean factors form an exceptional holomorphic--antiholomorphic family characterized, away from planar points, by harmonicity of $\log(|A|^2-H^2)$.
  The rigidity mechanism is governed by a Dirac discriminant $9c-4|A|^2$. Its hypothetical non-CMC real branch reduces to a spherical Gauss--Codazzi system whose exact Frobenius torsion is strictly negative. Model examples finally show that factorization of the geometric operator is distinct from the existence of a positive conformal mode. Thus the scalar--chiral channel is complete but too rigid to generate new non-CMC examples, providing a precise baseline for broader Clifford-valued constructions.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the long-time existence of the heterotic $G_2$ flow with bounded Ricci curvature, torsion tensor and dilaton. Besides, we also get the forward and backward uniqueness results for the solution.

### [19] [The isoperimetric inequality and CMC hypersurfaces in Cartan-Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20517)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard猜想长期未解，此前仅在n=2、n=3、n=4及部分情形得到验证，一般维度下缺乏对欧氏等周不等式的证明，且自由边界/奇异情形处理困难，因此需要在更大维度范围内给出统一处理。

Method: 核心是证明常平均曲率超曲面的一个精确不等式：通过对边界点对的积分，结合沿边界测地弦的Jacobi场估计；积分权重依赖弦长及其与边界的夹角，且逐维选取，并引入Green函数极点。该不等式可推广到被测地球所困的等周区域边界（曲率仅在自由部分为常数，可能带奇异点），最后用Kleiner与Ghomi-Spruck的等周剖面论证完成。

Result: 证得完备单连通非正截面曲率黎曼n-流形（3≤n≤9）中的精确欧氏等周不等式，即Cartan-Hadamard猜想在3≤n≤9成立；所得不等式对被测地球困住的等周区域边界仍成立。

Conclusion: Cartan-Hadamard猜想在3≤n≤9维度上成立；方法结合常平均曲率超曲面精确不等式、Jacobi场估计与等周剖面论证，为更高维情形提供了可能的技术路径。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $n$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, $3\leq n\leq9$, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in these dimensions. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, together with estimates for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords of the boundary. The weights in these integrals depend on the length of the chord and its angles with the boundary, and are chosen dimension by dimension, with a Green function pole. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions trapped in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part and which may have singularities. The isoperimetric-profile argument of Kleiner and Ghomi-Spruck completes the proof.

</details>


### [20] [Deformations of harmonic maps with conical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20588)
*Dominik Gutwein,Thibault Langlais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有孤立奇点、切映射光滑且多项式衰减的圆锥奇异调和映射的形变理论，证明在指标、零性与加权核非退化条件下其在背景度量小扰动下保持，并构造出满足假设的具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射在度量扰动下的稳定性（persistence）是几何分析中的核心问题，而带孤立奇点的调和映射（圆锥奇异情形）的形变理论尚不完整，需要建立相应框架并给出可验证的实例。

Method: 在紧致黎曼流形间考察圆锥奇异调和映射：切映射光滑、对切映射的衰减为多项式。对切映射的 Morse 指标与零性施加条件，并对 Jacobi 算子的加权核给出非退化假设，据此证明该类调和映射在背景度量小 C² 扰动下持续存在。进而验证径向投影与复 Hopf 纤维化满足切映射条件，并用显式构造得到圆锥奇异调和映射实例。

Result: 证明径向投影 R^m\{0}→S^{m-1}（m≥4）与复 Hopf 纤维化 C^{n+1}\{0}→CP^n（n≥1）满足所需的切映射条件；构造出 S^m\{N,S}→S^{m-1}、S^4\{N,S}→S^2 与 CP^2\{[0:0:1]}→CP^1 的显式圆锥奇异调和映射，在域上适当度量与靶的圆度量下满足形变定理假设。

Conclusion: 建立了圆锥奇异调和映射的形变定理，并给出满足假设的非平凡例子，说明该类奇点结构在度量扰动下具有稳定性。

Abstract: We study the deformation theory of harmonic maps with isolated singularities between compact Riemannian manifolds, in the case where all the tangent maps are smooth (away from the origin) and the decay to the tangents is polynomial. We will refer to these as conically singular harmonic maps. Under certain conditions on the Morse index and the nullity of the tangent maps and a non-degeneracy assumption on a weighted kernel of the Jacobi operator, we prove that such harmonic maps persist under a small $C^2$-perturbation of the background metric.

We first prove that the desired conditions on the tangent maps are satisfied by the radial projections $\mathbb{R}^m \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$ ($m \geq 4$) and the complex Hopf fibrations $\mathbb{C}^{n+1} \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{CP}^{n}$ ($n \geq 1$). The construction starts from an idea of three of the authors, who in \cite{DHS} tried to build a similar example with $C^{m-1, α}$ regularity for $g$. ### [24] [Generic Uniqueness of Isoperimetric Regions in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20790)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等周问题中极小化区域是否唯一是核心问题。经典结果常依赖于边界正则性或 Jacobi 算子的非退化性等假设，这限制了对可能出现奇异边界的等周区域的适用性。作者希望在一般度量下、无需这些额外假设地建立唯一性。

Method: 变分方法。在闭连通光滑流形 M^{n+1}（n≥1）上，对每个 s∈(0,1)\{1/2} 处理体积约束下的等周区域；对 s=1/2 单独分析。证明不要求边界正则性，也不要求约束 Jacobi 算子非退化。并将结论推广到固定体积 m 的一族度量以及 (g,m) 对的族。

Result: 对每个 s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，体积为 s·Vol_g(M) 的等周区域在一般光滑黎曼度量集合上唯一。半体积时，一般度量恰有两个极小化区域 E 和 E^c。推论：对任意 m>0，唯一性对满足 m<Vol_g(M) 的一般度量集合成立；对满足 0<m<Vol_g(M) 的一般 (g,m) 对也成立。结果适用于等周边界奇异的度量。

Conclusion: 等周区域唯一性是“一般”度量性质：除半体积外唯一，半体积时为互补二重。变分证明弱化了对边界正则性和 Jacobi 算子非退化的依赖，从而覆盖边界奇异的情形。

Abstract: Let $M^{n+1}$ be a closed, connected smooth manifold, where $n\geq 1$. For each prescribed volume fraction $s\in(0,1)\setminus\{\frac12\}$, we prove that the isoperimetric region of volume $s \operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$ is unique for a generic set of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$. At half volume, a generic metric has exactly two minimizers, a region $E$ and its complement $E^c$. As consequences, for every $m>0$, uniqueness holds for a generic set of metrics $g$ satisfying $m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$, and it also holds for a generic set of pairs $(g,m)$ with $0<m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$.

</details>

Result: 得到相对误差 o(1) 且在乘法常数层面显式的渐近公式；对 (r,s)=(2,3) 给出 ω^{ω^2} 以下序数素数编码的计数结果。

Conclusion: 该公式解决了 Vernaeve、Vindas、Weiermann 计数问题中 (r,s)=(2,3) 的情形，并在更一般的乘法独立整数对上给出显式渐进估计。

Abstract: Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime, and let $r,s\ge2$ be fixed multiplicatively independent integers. We count the integers up to $x$ whose prime factors all have the form $p_{r^a s^b}$ with integers $a,b\ge0$. Our asymptotic formula for this count has relative error $o(1)$ and is explicit down to the multiplicative constant. For $(r,s)=(2,3)$ these integers are the prime codes of the ordinals below $ω^{ω^2}$, so the formula settles that case of the counting problem of Vernaeve, Vindas and Weiermann.

Abstract: In recent work, Hirschhorn and the second author defined $a_k(n)$ to be the number of partitions of $n$ wherein the even parts come in only one color, while the odd parts may be ``colored'' with one of $k$ colors for fixed $k\geq 1$. This function generalizes the classical partition function and has been of significant interest because it satisfies a number of congruences. Although prior work studying $a_k(n)$ has resulted in congruences in arithmetic progressions in a somewhat ad hoc manner, this work gives a uniform framework for studying congruences modulo 3. This allows us to prove an infinite family of infinite families of non-nested congruences modulo 3.

For $q>2$ and odd $n\ge5$, we also prove that the Beelen--Montanucci curves are not covered by the Hermitian curve, extending the known result for Galois coverings. ### [29] [Simultaneous nonvanishing of quadratic twists via Rankin-Cohen brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19649)
*Ramin Takloo-Bighash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了迹化的对角Rankin--Cohen括号 $\mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e}$（$1\le e\le r$）在 $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ 中线性无关，由此导出至少 $r$ 个一阶 Hecke 特征形式其 $L(f\otimes\chi_D,\ell)$ 中心值非零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高阶（weight aspect）水平一 Hecke 特征形式的中心 $L$-值非零问题，特别是希望固定任意 $r$、在权充分大时得到至少 $r$ 个非零中心值的特征形式，并推广到带nebentypus 的判别式 $D$ 情形。

Result: 对充分大的 $\ell$（满足 $(-1)^\ell D>0$），前 $r$ 个迹化对角 Rankin--Cohen 括号在 $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ 中线性无关；从而至少 $r$ 个归一化 Hecke 特征形式 $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ 满足 $L(f\otimes\chi_D,\ell)\neq 0$。特例 $D=1$：对每个固定 $r$ 与充分大且 $K\equiv 0\pmod 4$ 的 $K$，至少有 $r$ 个权 $K$ 的水平一特征形式具有非零中心值。

Conclusion: 通过 Rankin--Cohen 括号的线性无关性结合 Petersson 公式，建立了权方向上一阶特征形式中心 $L$-值非零的下界，推广了 $D=1$ 的经典情形。

Abstract: Let $D$ be an odd fundamental discriminant, with $D=1$ permitted, and let $r\geq 1$ be fixed. We prove that, for every sufficiently large integer $\ell$ satisfying $(-1)^\ell D>0$, the first $r$ traced diagonal Rankin--Cohen brackets \[
  \mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e},
  \qquad 1\leq e\leq r, \] are linearly independent in $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$. Here $G_{k,D}$ is the Eisenstein series of weight $k$, level $|D|$, and nebentypus $χ_D$. The Petersson formula of Kayath, Lane, Neifeld, Ni, and Xue then implies that at least $r$ normalized Hecke eigenforms $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ satisfy $L(f\otimesχ_D,\ell)\neq 0$. For $D=1$, this gives, for every fixed $r$ and every sufficiently large $K\equiv 0\pmod 4$, at least $r$ level-one eigenforms of weight $K$ with nonzero central value.

</details>

Abstract: In the present paper, we study finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields from the point of view of mono-anabelian geometry. Abstract: We study logarithmic Mellin integrals attached to a reciprocal quadratic rational function. Throughout the saddle region $c>1$, $-2\sqrt c<b<0$, positive hyperbolic formulas for real $0<a<1$ give determinate Hausdorff moment sequences, strict total positivity, and monotone quotient limits. Abstract: We present a localized rooted-operator argument for Weil positivity in the real odd logarithmic channel, retaining the polar rank-one term throughout. A central point in Gate II is to place the inherited parent response in the same post-old-core/common-cut primal form in which the arithmetic mismatch and folded scalar debit are charged. If $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$ is the surviving inherited pivot and $a_m$ the inherited coordinate of the Schur minimizer, then $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}a_m=ω^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$, so the inherited source-coupling contribution is the negative metric energy of that response. The aligned forcing is retained explicitly, and an outward-rounded finite computation together with the analytic tail gives $g^{M+}_k=\frac12+\log k-\frac{439}{250\log 2}\sum_m V_{k,m}-\frac52 W_k>0$ for $k\ge7$. The simultaneous common-cut theorem keeps the parent-ground and transverse budgets separate before the common infimum, while the fold identity leaves a non-negative remainder. Starting from the rigorously certified endpoint $Y=7$, Schur induction yields positivity at the integer endpoints, zero-extension gives non-negativity at every finite support radius, and closure together with the restricted odd Weil criterion yields the Riemann hypothesis. Short fantasy interludes provide only an expository map and may be omitted without changing any definition, lemma, theorem, or proof.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $\mathbb N$ denote the set of nonnegative integers. For integers $k>1$ and $t\ge1$, Yang and Chen defined $f_k(t)$ to be the number of sets $A\subseteq\mathbb N$ for which \[ R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(\mathbb N\setminus A,n) \] for all integers $n\ge t$, and asked whether $f_k(t)$ and $f_l(t)$ are eventually equal for any integers $k,l>1$. We prove that \[ f_k(t)\asymp_k \frac{2^t}{t^{k/2}}, \] which gives a negative answer to their problem.

</details>

Abstract: We establish product-profile anti-concentration, density, and Remez estimates for multi-affine polynomials on the cube. If $\cB$ has exactly $d$ blocks, then $$ w_d(\cB) = d\prod_{B\in\cB}\sqrt{\abs B}, $$ and products of centered block averages show that both the small-ball profile and the dependence on the full block-size vector are optimal, up to constants depending only on $d$.

Geom. Anal., 31:748-771, 2021].

First, we construct a family of uniformly discrete sets $Λ\subset \mathbb{R}$, of uniform density one, such that the exponential system $\{e^{2πiλx} : λ\in Λ\}$ is complete in $L^p(S)$ for every $1 \leq p < \infty$ and every measurable set $S \subset \mathbb{R}$ with $|S| < 1$. We also show that no set that is asymptotically integer can have this universality property. Additionally, for every $v \in (0,1)$, we construct a set of integer frequencies and uniform density $v$ whose exponential system is complete in $L^p(S)$ for every $1 \leq p < \infty$ and every measurable set $S \subset [0,1]$ with $|S| < v$. Finally, we prove that the Sobolev regularity condition $α> \frac12$ for the existence of uniformly discrete uniqueness sets for spectra with periodic weak gaps, considered by Olevskii and Ulanovskii, is sharp. Our findings admit extensions to higher dimensions and are verified in Lean.

Abstract: We study the Gabor frame properties of the rational window $$ g(x)=\frac{x^2-1}{(x^2+1)(x^2+4)(x^2+9)}, $$ whose poles occur in symmetric pairs. We prove that every lattice satisfying $0<αβ<1/3$ generates a frame, and we establish an additional frame region for $β\ge1$ and $1/3\le αβ<0.47373$. Furthermore, we show that the rational hyperbolas $αβ=\frac{p}{3p-1}$, for $p\ge2,$ are entirely contained within the frame set. In contrast, we construct explicit non-frame lattice points on the hyperbolas $$αβ\in\left\{\frac{1}{3},\frac{1}{2},\frac{2}{3},\frac{3}{4}\right\}.$$ Finally, for the density family $αβ=p/(p+1)$, we derive a symmetry reduction of the associated Zibulski-Zeevi matrix, providing numerical evidence for a richer structure of non-frame obstructions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: It is well known that every hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space whose spectrum has planar Lebesgue measure zero is normal. In particular, every compact hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space is normal. In this paper, we investigate analogous structural and spectral phenomena for so-called sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples on a complex Hilbert space $\mathcal H$, namely, commuting $d$-tuples ${\bf T}=(T_1, \ldots, T_d)$ satisfying $\sum_{j=1}^d [T^*_j, T_j] \geqslant 0$. We show that every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple of compact operators decomposes as the direct sum of a normal $d$-tuple and a quasinilpotent sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple. As a consequence, every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple on a finite-dimensional Hilbert space is normal. In contrast to the single-operator case, a sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple need not be normaloid. Nevertheless, we establish a multivariable analogue of Putnam's inequality for sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples with commuting imaginary parts. As a consequence, we prove that ${\bf T}$ is normal whenever the planar Lebesgue measure of $s(σ({\bf T}))$ is zero, where $σ({\bf T})$ denotes the Taylor spectrum of ${\bf T}$ and ${s}$ is the complex-linear polynomial with alternating coefficients $1$ and $i$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Cesàro算子在Hardy空间间的映射性质此前缺乏对全参数范围（尤其含 $H^\infty$ 端点及 $p>q$ 情形）的统一刻画；本文意图给出 $0<p,q\le\infty$ 的完整有界性分类，并揭示测度 $\mu$ 与算子性质之间的精确对应。

Method: 以 $[0,1)$ 上有限正Borel测度 $\mu$ 及 $\gamma>0$ 定义算子 $\mathcal{C}_{\mu,\gamma}$，其系数含Gamma函数核。将 $H^p\to H^q$ 有界性转化为对 $\mu$ 的Carleson型测度条件、积分条件 $\int_0^1(\mu([t,1))/(1-t)^{\gamma-1/r})^r\,dt/(1-t)<\infty$ 及系数条件 $\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{r\gamma-2}\mu_n^r<\infty$ 的等价刻画，并通过 $F_{\mu,\alpha}=\mathcal{C}_{\mu,\alpha}(1)$ 建立函数空间隶属关系。

Result: 对于 $0<p<q<\infty$、$0<p=q<1$、以及 $0<p\le1$ 且 $q=\infty$，$\mathcal{C}_{\mu,\gamma}:H^p\to H^q$ 有界当且仅当 $\mu$ 为 $(\gamma+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson测度，对 $\gamma$ 无额外限制。对于 $1\le q<p\le\infty$（令 $1/r=1/q-1/p$），有界与紧等价于上述积分条件，也等价于 $F_{\mu,\gamma}\in H^r$ 及系数级数收敛。对于 $1<p<\infty$ 到 $H^\infty$，有界与紧由移位条件 $F_{\mu,\gamma+1}\in H^{p'}$（$p'=p/(p-1)$）刻画。

Conclusion: 由此获得广义Cesàro算子 $\mathcal{C}_{\mu,\gamma}$ 在全范围 $0<p,q\le\infty$ 上Hardy空间间有界性的完整分类，统一了Carleson测度条件、积分/系数条件与 $H^r$ 隶属条件等不同刻画方式。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a finite positive Borel measure on $[0,1)$ and let $γ>0$. We establish sharp mapping criteria for the generalized Cesàro operator \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal C_{μ,γ}f(z)
  =\sum_{n=0}^\infty μ_n
  \left(\sum_{k=0}^n
  \frac{Γ(n-k+γ)}{Γ(γ)(n-k)!}a_k\right)z^n,\qquad z\in \mathbb{D},\end{equation*}
  between Hardy spaces, including the source and target $H^\infty$ endpoints. For $0<p<q<\infty$, for $0<p=q<1$, and for $0<p\le1$ with $q=\infty$, the boundedness of $ \mathcal C_{μ,γ}: H^p \to H^q$ is equivalent to $μ$ being a $(γ+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson measure, without further restrictions on $γ$. For $1\le q<p\le\infty$, set $1/r=1/q-1/p$. In this range, boundedness and compactness are equivalent to \[
  \int_0^1
  \left(\frac{μ([t,1))}{(1-t)^{γ-1/r}}\right)^r
  \frac{dt}{1-t}<\infty. \] This condition is also equivalent to $F_{μ,γ}\in H^r$ and to $\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{rγ-2}μ_n^r<\infty$, where $F_{μ,α}=\mathcal C_{μ,α}(1)$ and $μ_n=\int_{[0,1)}t^n\,dμ(t)$. For $1<p<\infty$, boundedness and compactness from $H^p$ to $H^\infty$ are characterized by the shifted condition $F_{μ,γ+1}\in H^{p'}$, where $p'=p/(p-1)$. We therefore obtain a complete boundedness classification of the generalized Cesàro operators $\mathcal C_{μ,γ}$ between Hardy spaces $H^p$ and $H^q$ for the full range $0<p,q\le\infty$.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a general, nonsymmetric Ornstein--Uhlenbeck semigroup $(\mathcal H_t)_{t>0}$. Abstract: We give a computer-assisted proof that the universal real Grothendieck constant satisfies $K_G^{\mathbb R}\le 1.7813$. A sufficient inverse-majorant inequality is certified by enclosing the scalar coefficient head through degree $301$ and bounding the entire remaining tail using a weighted Gaussian trace estimate. The finite integral is bounded on a complete interval partition; its spatial exterior is controlled analytically. Exact parameters and source code that regenerate all accepted numerical inputs accompany the paper. Method: 论文结合了两个关键工具：奇数维情形下的变分迹不等式，以及偶数维情形下关于中心化矩阵Moore--Penrose逆的尖锐范围约束估计。

Result: 成功证明了$S$-矩阵猜想，建立了$\|A^{-1}\|_F\ge 2n/(n+1)$的下界，并刻画了等号成立条件为$A$是$S$-矩阵。

Conclusion: $S$-矩阵猜想得证，完善了该领域长期存在的公开问题。

Abstract: The $S$-matrix conjecture was formulated by Sloane and Harwit in 1976, motivated by an $A$-optimal design problem arising in spectroscopy. It states that if $A$ is a nonsingular real $n\times n$ matrix whose entries lie in $[0,1]$, then $$\left\|A^{-1}\right\|_F\ge\frac{2n}{n+1}.$$ Moreover, equality holds if and only if $A$ is an $S$-matrix. In this paper, we prove the conjecture by combining a variational trace inequality in odd dimensions with a sharp range-constrained estimate for the Moore--Penrose inverse of a centered matrix in even dimensions.

</details>


### [66] [Lévy measures for Dirichlet-type spaces on the unit bidisc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20346)

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在单位双圆盘上的 Dirichlet 型空间 D(ρ^(1),ρ^(2)) 中，给出了与完全交错多序列 {‖z^α‖²} 相关联的 Lévy 测度 ν_(M_z,1) 在 [0,1]²\{(1,1)} 上的显式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirichlet 型空间上乘法算子的谱与完全交错多序列的 Lévy 测度刻画是算子理论中的核心问题，但双圆盘情形下的显式 Lévy 测度表达此前尚不明确，作者希望将单变量结果推广到双变量并给出具体测度公式。

Method: 在单位双圆盘 D² 上定义 Dirichlet 型空间 D(ρ^(1),ρ^(2))，其中 ρ^(i) 为闭单位圆盘上的有限正 Borel 测度；考察与完全交错多序列 {‖z^α‖²}_{α∈Z_+²} 相关联的 Lévy 测度 ν_(M_z,1)，利用推前测度 S_*ρ^(i)（由 S(z)=|z|² 诱导）及 Dirac 测度 δ_1 进行显式构造与推导，并验证所得测度满足完全交错性条件。

Result: 证明在 [0,1]²\{(1,1)} 上成立显式表示：dν_(M_z,1)(x) = (1/(1-x₁)) d((S_*ρ^(1))×δ₁)(x) + (1/(1-x₂)) d(δ₁×(S_*ρ^(2)))(x)，即 Lévy 测度分解为分别关于第一、第二坐标的两项奇异测度之和，其密度因子为 1/(1-x_i)。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了双圆盘 Dirichlet 型空间关联 Lévy 测度的精确显式刻画，揭示了其结构由推前测度 S_*ρ^(i) 与沿坐标方向的 Dirac 测度直接决定，为多变量算子谱与完全交错多序列理论提供了新的具体工具。

Abstract: For the Dirichlet-type space $\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})$ on the unit bidisc $\mathbb D^2,$ where $ρ^{(1)}$ and $ρ^{(2)}$ are finite positive Borel measures on the closed unit disc $\overline{\mathbb D},$ we show that the Lévy measure $ν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$ associated with the completely alternating multisequence $\left\{\|z^α\|_{\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})}^2 \right\}_{α\in\mathbb Z_+^2}$ admits the explicit representation
  \begin{equation*}
  dν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}(x)=\frac{1}{1-x_1}d((S_{*}ρ^{(1)})\timesδ_1)(x)+\frac{1}{1-x_2}d(δ_1\times (S_{*}ρ^{(2)}))(x)
  \end{equation*} 
  on $[0,1]^2\backslash\{(1,1)\},$ where $S_{*}ρ^{(i)},$ $i=1,2$, denotes the pushforward measure of $ρ^{(i)}$ by the map $S:\overline{\mathbb D}\rightarrow [0,1]$ defined by $S(z)=|z|^2,$ $z\in \overline{\mathbb D},$ and $δ_1$ denotes the Dirac measure at 1.

</details>

For $n\ge2$, we construct two bounded nonradial Schwartz symbols on $\mathbb C^n$ whose Toeplitz operators are nonzero and have zero product on $F^2(\mathbb C^n)$. For $n=1$ and each $c\in(1/2,1)$, we construct two smooth nonradial symbols of growth at most $Ce^{c|z|^2}$ for some constant $C>0$. Their extended Toeplitz operators in $F^2(\mathbb C)$ are nonzero and have zero product on all holomorphic polynomials. Moreover, the second symbol is bounded when $c\geq3/4$. Our proofs use Gaussian kernel calculations, matrix identities, theta functions and Fourier transform.

### [69] [Normal-Direction Energy and Fourier Restriction for Convex Planar Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20643)
*Vicente Vergara*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文通过Gauss映射组织Fourier延拓中的质量分布，引入由曲率测度决定的内在终端切向尺度与角分辨率，建立局部$L^4$延拓估计与加权变体，并对单项曲线给出显式终端尺度与临界Frostman阈值$s_c(k)=\frac{k-2}{3k-4}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fourier延拓估计通常依赖全局曲率或平坦性假设，难以精细刻画不同位置、不同法方向上的局部分辨率与能量分布。本文希望借助Gauss映射与曲率测度，为凸$C^2$平面曲线的延拓问题提供位置相关（position-dependent）的尺度分析框架，并统一处理平坦点与非退化区域。

Method: 1) 用Gauss映射$N$将曲面测度$|f|^2\mathrm d\sigma$推前为法向测度$\nu_f=N_\#(|f|^2\mathrm d\sigma)$，以刻画法向质量分布；2) 由转动测度$\mathrm d\mu_\kappa=\kappa\,\mathrm d\sigma$定义终端切向尺度$r_R(\xi)$，满足$r_R(\xi)\mu_\kappa(B_\Gamma(\xi,r_R(\xi)))\asymp R^{-1}$，并给出角分辨率$\rho_R(\xi)=R^{-1}/r_R(\xi)$；3) 在$\mu_\kappa$满足doubling假设下，证明$r_R$（至结构常数）是曲线可线性化到精度$R^{-1}$的最大尺度；4) 基于这些尺度构造法向能量及终端分解与横向几何，导出局部$L^4$延拓估计和加权版本；5) 对单项曲线$\gamma_k(t)=(t,t^k)$显式求解终端尺度，并用$\nu_f$的角关联给出能量的多尺度表示，在$s$维Frostman条件下得到增长图。

Result: - 建立了基于Gauss映射推前测度与转动测度的终端尺度理论，在doubling假设下$r_R$为最大线性化尺度，并由此得到局部$L^4$Fourier延拓估计及加权变体。
- 对单项曲线$\gamma_k(t)=(t,t^k)$（$k\geq3$实），终端尺度显式可得，能量具有用$\nu_f$角关联表示的多尺度形式。
- 在$\nu_f$满足$s$维Frostman条件时，得到增长图与临界阈值$s_c(k)=\frac{k-2}{3k-4}$，区分平坦点区域与非退化区域；所得速率（含临界对数修正）在能量层面是sharp的。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一套由Gauss映射与曲率测度驱动的终端尺度与法向能量框架，统一刻画凸$C^2$平面曲线Fourier延拓中的位置相关分辨率，并给出局部$L^4$估计。对单项曲线，该框架给出显式终端尺度、能量多尺度表示以及临界阈值$s_c(k)$，且在能量层面达到最优，说明平坦点与非退化机制之间存在清晰的相变分界。

Abstract: We study Fourier extension from compact convex $C^2$ planar curves by organizing the mass $|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ$ through the Gauss map. The pushforward measure \[ ν_f= N_\#\bigl(|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ\bigr) \] records its distribution in normal directions. The turning measure $\mathrm dμ_κ=κ\,\mathrm dσ$ determines an intrinsic terminal tangential scale $r_R(ξ)$ through \[ r_R(ξ)\, μ_κ\bigl(B_Γ(ξ,r_R(ξ))\bigr) \asymp R^{-1}, \] and hence a position-dependent angular resolution $ρ_R(ξ)=R^{-1}/r_R(ξ)$. Under a doubling hypothesis on $μ_κ$, $r_R$ is, up to structural constants, the largest scale on which the curve can be linearized to precision $R^{-1}$. These scales define a normal-direction energy, and the associated terminal decomposition and transverse geometry yield local $L^4$ Fourier extension estimates and weighted variants.

Under an $s$-dimensional Frostman condition on $ν_f$, this yields a growth diagram with critical threshold \[ s_c(k)=\frac{k-2}{3k-4}, \] separating the flat-point and nondegenerate regimes. The resulting rates, including the critical logarithmic correction, are sharp at the energy level.

</details>

*Frédéric Protin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 提出基于嵌套再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的统一监督/无监督学习框架，用于密度估计与二分类，并证明估计量的几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定RKHS中，统计学习的收敛结果受限于核的逼近能力；而直接在全$L^2$空间中学习又缺乏RKHS的计算结构。因此需要一个既能保留RKHS计算便利性、又具备全$L^2$逼近能力的统一框架。

Method: 引入嵌套RKHS概念：一列递增的RKHS，其并集在环境$L^2$空间中稠密。先在固定RKHS中建立连续嵌入到$L^2$的随机元素经验平均的强收敛结果；再利用嵌套结构与并集的稠密性，对密度估计构造目标密度在各级RKHS上正交投影的经验估计量并采用对角选择程序；对二分类将Bayes目标的逼近表述为总体/经验风险泛函的最小化问题。

Result: 密度估计中，经验估计量几乎必然收敛，经对角选择得到的估计量序列在$L^2$中几乎必然收敛到目标密度。二分类中，证明了经验最小化子的相合性，并借助嵌套结构获得向Bayes目标在$L^2$中的收敛；同时给出基于环境$L^2$空间中超平面与加权距离的几何解释，并指出总体风险可用误分类概率进行概率解释。

Conclusion: 嵌套RKHS为监督与无监督学习提供了统一的理论框架，成功将固定RKHS的统计收敛结果与全$L^2$空间的逼近能力相结合。

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for supervised and unsupervised learning based on reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). We introduce the notion of a nested RKHS, namely an increasing sequence of RKHSs whose union is dense in an ambient $L^2$-space. This construction makes it possible to exploit the computational structure of RKHS methods at each finite approximation level while retaining the approximation power of the full $L^2$-space.
  We first study statistical learning in a fixed RKHS. In both unsupervised and supervised settings, we establish strong convergence results for empirical averages of random elements taking values in an RKHS continuously embedded in $L^2$. We then use the nested RKHS structure to combine these statistical convergence results with the density of the union of the RKHSs in the ambient $L^2$-space. We apply this construction to probability density estimation and binary Bayesian classification. For density estimation, we construct explicit empirical estimators of the orthogonal projections of the target density onto the RKHSs and establish their almost sure convergence. A diagonal selection procedure then yields a sequence of estimators converging almost surely to the target density in $L^2$.
  For binary Bayesian classification, we formulate the approximation of the Bayes target as the minimization of suitable population and empirical risk functionals. We prove consistency of the empirical minimizers and use the nested structure to obtain convergence towards the Bayes target in $L^2$. We further provide a geometric interpretation in terms of hyperplanes and weighted distances in the ambient $L^2$-space. Finally, we show that the population risk admits a direct probabilistic interpretation in terms of the probability of misclassification.

</details>

Abstract: Building on the point interaction method developed in our previous work, this paper studies inverse eigenvalue problems for regular Sturm-Liouville problems with Dirac weights. More precisely, we explicitly reconstruct the potential of regular Sturm-Liouville problems with the single-point and two-point Dirac weights from the solutions of a class of Cauchy problems which is completely determined by the first eigenvalues of a family of perturbed problems originating from moving point interaction models in quantum mechanics. Finally, the multi-point Dirac weighted case is also discussed.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Amghibech (2003)提出了有限图谱全谱是否有限的问题，此前该问题未被解决。作者希望确定有限图的图$p$-拉普拉斯算子全谱的有限性，并进一步理解谱基数如何随图规模增长、其特征簇的拓扑复杂度，以及在$p=2$与$p\neq2$时的差异。

Method: 证明结合了o-minimal几何与Pfaffian几何、射影$L^p$-对偶、Morse理论、临界群以及张量特征值界；对带符号加权图$p$-Schrödinger算子（任意实势）建立统一系数与统一$p$的界；对均匀加权$K_n$，将非平凡特征线与$\mathbb{RP}^{n-2}$坐标超平面胞腔分解的重心对应，并在$p=2$两侧计算局部Morse数据，通过重整化对数极限描述$p=2$处的过渡。

Abstract: We prove that the full spectrum of the graph $p$-Laplacian is finite for every finite graph and every real $p>1$. This resolves the problem of finiteness of the full graph spectrum posed by Amghibech (2003). More generally, for signed weighted graph $p$-Schrödinger operators with arbitrary real potentials, we obtain bounds in terms of the numbers of vertices and positive-weight edges, uniform in all coefficients and in $p$. On $n$ vertices with $m$ positive-weight edges, the logarithms of the spectral cardinality and of the total number of connected components of the normalised eigenvector sets are $O((n+m)^2)$; the sum over the full spectrum of their rational Betti numbers is at most $\exp(C(n+m)^2)$ for an absolute constant $C$. These topological bounds extend to homogeneous eigenproblems built from arbitrary finite families of linear forms, including generalised $p$-eigenvalue problems for matrix pairs. For the uniformly weighted $K_n$ and $p\ne2$, we identify the nonconstant eigenlines with the barycentres of the coordinate-hyperplane cell decomposition of $\mathbb RP^{n-2}$ and determine their local Morse data on either side of $p=2$. A renormalised logarithmic limit describes the transition at $p=2$. Positive integer weights then separate these critical values, giving at least $(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2$ distinct spectral values for every fixed $p\ne2$ and every $n\geq2$. By contrast, the maximal spectral cardinality at $p=2$ is $n$; at $p=4$, the maximal spectral cardinalities in the unsigned and general classes both have exponential growth rate exactly $3$. For $n\geq3$ the same examples resolve Amghibech's extremal question. The proof combines o-minimal and Pfaffian geometry with projective $L^p$-duality, Morse theory, critical groups, and tensor eigenvalue bounds.

</details>


### [73] [Essential spectral geometry of the Maxwell system in unbounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20339)
*Francesco Ferraresso,Marco Marletta*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究无界区域上无散矢量场中curl curl算子的本质谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析curl curl算子在无界区域上的本质谱，理解区域几何（拟锥、拟柱、喇叭形等）如何影响本质谱结构，尤其是体积与边界平均凸性对谱的影响。

Method: 将curl curl算子限制在无散矢量场上，分析拟锥域和拟柱域、圆形截面喇叭域、环形截面喇叭域以及穿孔指数喇叭等具体区域的本质谱；对环形截面腔道，利用横向法向调和场构造有效一维Schrödinger算子H_V，并通过比较其本质谱来刻画σ_e(M)。

Result: 拟锥域中σ_e(M)=[0,+∞)，拟柱域中σ_e(M)≠∅；对圆截面且边界最终平均凸的喇叭域，σ_e(M)=∅且与体积无关；对环形截面喇叭，σ_e(M)⊇σ_e(H_V)；对双指数孔的穿孔指数喇叭族，随孔在无穷处的收缩速率不同，分别有σ_e(M)=∅、σ_e(M)=[γ²,+∞)或σ_e(M)=[0,+∞)。

Conclusion: curl curl算子在无界区域上的本质谱由区域的无穷远处几何与边界平均凸性决定，而非体积；穿孔结构及孔的收缩速率可导致本质谱空、半直线或含间段的谱结构。

Abstract: We analyse the essential spectrum of ${\mathcal M} = {\operatorname{curl}} {\operatorname{curl}}$ acting on divergence-free vector fields in unbounded domains of $\mathbb{R}^3$. We show that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$ in quasi-conical domains and $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \neq \emptyset$ in quasi-cylindrical domains. For horn-shaped domains with circular cross-section and eventually mean-convex boundary, we establish that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, independently of their volume. For horns with annular cross-section, the transverse normal harmonic field determines an effective one-dimensional Schrödinger operator $H_V$ with $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \supseteq σ_e(H_V)$. Finally, for a concrete family of perforated exponential horns with a double-exponential hole, we show that depending on the rate of shrinking of the hole at infinity, either $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [γ^2, +\infty)$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$.

</details>

*Khalid Bdarneh*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究径向加权Fock空间上Toeplitz算子的可交换族与C*-代数结构，利用表示论工具刻画了U(n)子群不变符号生成C*-代数的可交换性，并给出特征值的积分表示及关于平方根度量的非一致连续性反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画加权Fock空间上Toeplitz算子所生成C*-代数的可交换性，是理解其谱结构与算子代数性质的核心问题；已有结果多针对特定对称群或符号，缺乏对一般紧子群H⊆U(n)的统一刻画。本文旨在以表示论为工具，系统处理U_m不变符号、k-准径向符号及一般紧子群不变符号的Toeplitz算子代数，并进一步探讨径向权增长下特征值序列的连续性。

Method: 采用表示论方法：将U_m=U(b_1)×…×U(b_k)实现为U(n)的分块对角子群，分解加权Fock空间为不可约U_m-模；通过该分解研究U_m不变符号与k-准径向符号的Toeplitz算子，给出其特征值的显式积分表示。对任意紧子群H⊆U(n)，以表示π|_H的多重性无关性（multiplicity-free）为判据刻画H不变Toeplitz算子生成C*-代数的可交换性。针对对数增长的径向权，构造有界径向符号并分析其对应特征值序列关于平方根度量的性质。

Result: 1) 给出U_m不变符号与k-准径向符号的Toeplitz算子特征值的显式积分表示；2) 对任意紧子群H⊆U(n)，证明H不变Toeplitz算子生成C*-代数可交换当且仅当表示π|_H是多重性无关的；3) 对对数增长径向权，构造出有界径向符号，使其特征值序列关于平方根度量非一致连续，从而该特征值序列集的均匀闭包不等于关于平方根度量一致有界的序列C*-代数。

Conclusion: U(n)子群不变Toeplitz算子代数的可交换性可由表示的多重性无关性统一刻画；通过群分解可得到特征值的积分表示。对于对数增长径向权，特征值序列的均匀闭包严格小于相应一致连续序列C*-代数，揭示径向权增长对谱性质的影响。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz operators acting on radial weighted Fock spaces. We use tools from representation theory to construct commutative families of $C^*$-algebras that are generated by Toeplitz operators whose symbols are invariant under the action of $\U(n)$. For a partition $m=(b_1,...,b_k)$ of an integer $n$, we realize \[\mathbf{\U_m}:=\U(b_1)\times...\times\U(b_k)\] as a block diagonally subgroup of $\U(n)$ and describe the decomposition of the weighted Fock space into irreducible $\mathbf{U_m}$-modules. This allow us to study Toeplitz operators with symbols that are invariant under $\mathbf{U_m}$ and $k$-quasi-radial symbols, and we provide and explicit integral representation of their eigenvalues. More generally, for an arbitrary compact subgroup $H\subseteq\U(n)$, we characterize the commutativity of the $C^*$-algebra generated by $H$-invariant Toeplitz operators in terms of the multiplicity-free property of the representation $π|_H$. Finally, for a logarithmically growing radial weight, we construct a bounded radial symbol for which the corresponding eigenvalue sequence not uniformly continuous with respect to the square root metric. Consequently, the uniform closure of the set of eigenvalue sequences does not coincide with the $C^*$-algebra of bounded sequences that are uniformly continuous with respect to the square root metric.

</details>


### [75] [A Transfinite Christensen--Pedersen Argument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20718)
*Jananan Arulseelan*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文将Christensen-Pedersen关于真无限AW*-代数单调序列完备性的证明推广到任意基数κ：定义κ-齐次性并将结论提升为κ-单调完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christensen和Pedersen证明了每个真无限AW*-代数都是单调序列完备的。作者希望分离其证明中可推广到任意基数的部分，从而在更一般的基数κ下获得更广泛的单调完备性结论。

Method: 提取原证明所依赖的性质：存在一列相互正交、每个都等价于1且和为1的投影。将此推广定义为κ-齐次性（κ=ℵ₀时还原为上述性质）。κ-齐次性在每个后继阶段提供新的正交空间以进行超限投影膨胀；AW*-代数的正规性在每个极限阶段以及构造末尾，将投影的join转化为自伴序下的supremum，从而替代可数情形中的可加性定理与扰动论证。

Result: 证明了每个κ-齐次AW*-代数都是κ-单调完备的；并且κ-单调完备性连同κ个ordinary states能将正元素与零分离，可推出单调完备性。作为应用，若一个AW*-因子有一个角（corner）容许忠实态，则该因子是单调完备的。

Conclusion: 该工作把Christensen-Pedersen的结论从可数情形推广到任意基数κ，给出了κ-齐次性作为关键结构性条件，并利用AW*-代数的正规性替代了可加性定理与扰动论证，最终得到关于AW*-因子单调完备性的新应用。

Abstract: Christensen and Pedersen proved that every properly infinite $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebra is monotone sequentially complete. We isolate the part of their argument that extends to an arbitrary cardinal. The property of properly infinite $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebras used in their proof is the existence of an orthogonal sequence of projections, each equivalent to $1$, with sum $1$. We generalize this by defining a notion of $κ$-homogeneity, with the case $κ= \aleph_{0}$ recovering the aforementioned property. $κ$-homogeneity supplies the fresh orthogonal space needed at each successor stage of a transfinite projection dilation, and normality of $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebras supplies, at every limit stage and again at the end of the construction, the passage from a join of projections to a supremum in the self-adjoint order. This second point replaces both the addability theorem and the perturbation argument used in the countable case. We prove that every $κ$-homogeneous $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebra is $κ$-monotone complete, and that $κ$-monotone completeness together with $κ$ ordinary states separating positive elements from zero implies monotone completeness. As an application, an $\mathrm{AW}^*$-factor with a corner admitting a faithful state is monotone complete.

</details>

Result: 所构造的基于Connes融合的张量积与编织结构，与arXiv:2410.21454中用局部化可迁移自同态构造的范畴等价。

Conclusion: 作为应用，证明了arXiv:2506.19969中由酉编织融合范畴$\mathcal{B}$构造的二维编织量子自旋系统的超选择扇区范畴，作为编织$\mathrm{W^*}$-张量范畴等价于$\mathsf{Hilb}(\mathcal{B})^{\rm op}$，即$\mathcal{B}$的酉ind-完备化的对偶范畴。

Abstract: The article arXiv:2410.21454 constructed a braided $\mathrm{W^*}$-tensor category of superselection sectors associated to a net of von Neumann algebras on a suitably geometric poset using localized and transportable endomorphisms. In this article, we construct an equivalent tensor product and braiding using the Connes fusion tensor product without localizing, using ideas from arXiv:1812.04470 for conformal nets. As an application, we prove that the category of superselection sectors associated to the 2D braided quantum spin system constructed from a unitary braided fusion category $\mathcal{B}$ in arXiv:2506.19969 is equivalent to $\mathsf{Hilb}(\mathcal{B})^{\rm op}$, the opposite of the unitary ind-completion of $\mathcal{B}$, as a braided $\mathrm{W^*}$-tensor category.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We consider interaction functionals of the form \begin{equation*} ν\mapsto {\mathscr E}(ν)=\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N}\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} W(x-y)\intdν(y)\intdν(x)+\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} V(x)\intdν(x)\text{,} \end{equation*} involving an anisotropic kernel $W$ and a general confinement potential $V$. Under standard assumptions on $W$ and its Fourier transform, we show that the minimizer of ${\mathscr E}$ has $L^2$ density. Our argument relies on a careful analysis of nonlocal variational inequalities, which may be of independent interest.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate time-dependent semiclassical measures associated with the magnetic Schrödinger flow on the flat two-dimensional torus in long-time regimes. Under a geometric nonvanishing assumption on an effective magnetic force, we identify two thresholds and prove a dynamical form of quantum unique ergodicity for such systems. If the time scale $τ_h \gg h^{-1/2}$, then the configuration marginal is the normalized Lebesgue measure on $\mathbb T^2$. In addition, if $τ_h \gg h^{-1}$, then the momentum marginal is, conditionally on each regular level set, the normalized invariant measure on that curve.

Abstract: This work focuses on range characterization of spherical mean transform (SMT) in odd-dimension Euclidean space. In a recent work, a simpler description characterizing the range of SMT in odd dimensions was derived. The sufficiency part of this characterization was shown using an existing range characterization result due to Agranovsky-Kuchment-Quinto. In the current work, we provide a direct and constructive proof showing the sufficiency of the aforementioned symmetry conditions and we obtain this without invoking any of the existing range characterization results. As an added advantage, given a function $g$ satisfying the range conditions, our approach provides an explicit construction of a function $f$ such that the SMT of $f$ is $g$.

</details>

Result: 在 p≥4 的条件下，证明了非线性 FPKE 解测度的存在性，求得了相关鞅问题的解，并由此得到了无穷小生成元为 Δ_p+Δ_p^s 混合算子的 McKean–Vlasov 型带跳 SDE 的弱解存在性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过 PDE—FPKE—鞅问题—SDE 的链条，建立了由 p-Laplacian 与分数阶 p-Laplacian 混合算子驱动的带跳 McKean–Vlasov 随机过程的存在性，并提出了新的非线性叠加原理作为关键工具。

Abstract: We prove the existence of a McKean--Vlasov stochastic process with jumps associated to the nonlinear parabolic equation $\partial_t u = Δ_p u + Δ_p^s u$ in $\R^N\times(0,\infty)$, where $Δ_p$ is the $p$-Laplacian and $Δ_p^s$ is the fractional $p$-Laplacian. The algorithm used is the following : first, after proving the existence of a solution for the PDE presented earlier, we rewrite it as a nonlinear Fokker-Planck-Kolmogorov equation whose solution-measure is guaranted when $p\ge4$. Then we solve the martingale problem associated to our FPKE via a new nonlinear supersition principle. Finally, thanks to the martingale solution obtained, we derive the existence of a weak solution for the McKean-Vlasov's type SDE with jumps whose infinitesimal generator is a << hybrid version >> of the operator $Δ_p+Δ_p^s$.

</details>


### [83] [Onsager's Conjecture for Ideal Magnetohydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19506)
*Matteo Giardi,László Székelyhidi*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 对理想MHD方程构造了耗散能量、交叉螺旋度但磁螺旋度非平凡的C^γ弱解，并建立各向异性Hölder界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究理想磁流体力学（MHD）方程弱解的非唯一性与奇异耗散现象，将流体中的凸积分方法推广到MHD，并衔接Goldreich–Sridhar与Iroshnikov–Kraichnan湍流标度图像。

Method: 在体积保持微分同胚群（Arnold意义下）的李代数层面，结合经典凸积分技术与几何构造；对速度与磁场分别设定各向异性Hölder指数，并施加沿磁力线方向更强的正则性。

Result: 对任意0≤γ<1/3构造出v,B∈C^γ(T^3×R)的弱解，既不守恒总能量也不守恒交叉螺旋度，同时具有非平凡磁螺旋度；得到速度与磁场指数可不同的各向异性Hölder界及沿每条磁力线更强正则性；允许指数关联1/3、1/2与1/4标度。该工作显著推广Enciso–Peñafiel-Tomás–Peralta-Salas的结果，并可在不用Newton迭代的情况下重证Giri–Radu的结论。

Conclusion: 凸积分与几何方法可有效处理理想MHD弱解的奇异性与耗散，并统一若干湍流标度；该方法为MHD弱解非唯一性及其螺旋度行为提供了更强框架。

Abstract: For any $0\leγ<1/3$ we construct weak solutions $(v,B,p)$ of the ideal MHD equations with $v,B\in C^γ(\mathbb T^3\times\mathbb R)$, which conserve neither the total energy nor the cross-helicity and have nontrivial magnetic helicity. We also establish anisotropic Hölder bounds with distinct velocity and magnetic exponents and stronger regularity along every magnetic field line. The admissible exponents connect the Goldreich--Sridhar \cite{GoldreichSridhar1995} spatial and parallel pair $1/3, 1/2$ with the $1/4$ spatial scaling of the Iroshnikov--Kraichnan weak-turbulence picture \cite{Iroshnikov1963,Kraichnan1965}. In the spirit of Arnold's formulation of ideal hydrodynamics, a solution is regarded as a path of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms; the proof is then based on the interplay between classical convex integration techniques and geometric constructions at the level of the Lie algebra of this Lie group. Our work substantially extends the recent result of Enciso, Peñafiel-Tomás and Peralta-Salas \cite{EnPePe} and can be used to reprove \cite{GiRa} of Giri and Radu without a Newton iteration.

</details>


### [84] [Global Minimality of Matrix-Valued Heteroclinic Connections between Vacuum Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19590)
*Sisi Guan,Yu Cheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究真空集为连续共轭轨道的矩阵值异宿连接的全局能量极小化，证明两个参数区间的SU(5)×Z2模型中非阿贝尔扭结为全局极小元，并给出精确壁张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵值多势阱异宿连接研究多假设真空为孤立点或引入对角性、交换性、对称性约化等限制。本文旨在处理真空集为连续共轭轨道的非孤立情形，并在不施加此类约化的条件下获得全局极小性的严格结论。

Method: 首先刻画四次势的完整零点集为两个紧致齐次真空轨道的无交并；构造以到真空轨道的Frobenius距离为控制量的辅助势，通过逐点比较原势、标定（calibration）不等式与一维余面积公式，导出任意有限能量连接的尖锐下界。证明分离为模型无关的距离-余面积判据与模型相关的轨道几何、多项式正性估计两部分。

Result: 得到连接两真空分量的每个有限能量连接的尖锐下界，两个显式非阿贝尔扭结达到该下界，从而在全轨道到轨道异宿类中为全局极小元，其精确壁张力分别为4√2 μ³/(3λ) 与 9√2 μ³/(10h)。等价的，相应直线段路径实现真空流形间的退化加权距离。对任意固定端点，能量下确界等于同一壁张力，且当且仅当端点构成两轨道的最邻近点对时可达；非最邻近端点扇区因沿真空流形的切向运动渐慢而不可达。

Conclusion: 本文建立了处理对称性生成真空的矩阵值多势阱势的通用机制，将模型无关的距离-余面积判据与具体轨道几何分离，可复用于更广泛的矩阵值异宿问题。

Abstract: We study global energy minimization for matrix-valued heteroclinic connections whose vacuum sets are continuous conjugacy orbits rather than isolated points. For two distinguished parameter regimes of a one-dimensional $\mathrm{SU}(5)\times\mathbb Z_2$ matrix field model, we first identify the full zero set of the quartic potential as the disjoint union of two compact homogeneous vacuum orbits. We then construct auxiliary potentials controlled by the Frobenius distance to these orbits. Pointwise comparison with the original potential, combined with a calibration inequality and the one-dimensional coarea formula, gives a sharp lower bound for every finite-energy connection joining the two vacuum components; no diagonality, commutativity, or symmetry-reduced ansatz is imposed on the competitors. Two explicit non-Abelian kinks attain the bound and are therefore global minimizers in the full orbit-to-orbit heteroclinic classes. Their exact wall tensions are $4\sqrt2\,μ^3/(3λ)$ and $9\sqrt2\,μ^3/(10h)$, respectively. Equivalently, the corresponding line-segment paths realize the degenerate weighted distance between the vacuum manifolds. We also resolve every fixed-endpoint sector: for arbitrary prescribed representatives on the two vacuum orbits, the energy infimum is the same wall tension, and it is attained if and only if the prescribed representatives form a closest pair of the two orbits. Nonclosest endpoint sectors therefore exhibit non-attainment through increasingly slow tangential motion along the vacuum manifolds. The proof separates a model-independent distance--coarea criterion from the model-specific orbit geometry and polynomial positivity estimates, providing a reusable mechanism for matrix-valued multiwell potentials with symmetry-generated vacua.

</details>

Abstract: We consider the elastic wave equation in three-dimensional isotropic medium with up to quadratic nonlinear terms. We show that five out of the six elastic parameters can be uniquely determined by boundary measurements under the assumption that the wavespeeds are non-trapping, while the last one can also be determined in a generic setting.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the fixed angle scattering problems raised by Rakesh and Salo. We show that a compactly supported potential is uniquely determined by the far field pattern at a fixed angle, which is an open problem. Our method is based on a novel Carleman estimate and the ideas introduced by Bukhgeim and Klibanov on the use of Carleman estimates for inverse problems.

</details>

*Shiguang Ma,Zijian Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在Carnot群上研究非负解的存在性与逐点Wolff位势界，方程包含指数型非线性项与Radon测度，分次临界与临界两种情形分别处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Carnot群的次Laplace型方程中，含指数非线性与测度数据的解的存在性及定量估计尚不完善，需要在次临界与临界情形下建立统一的存在性与位势刻画。

Method: 针对$-Δ_{\mathbb{G},p}u=H_l(αu^β)+μ$，其中$H_l(t)=e^t-\sum_{j=0}^{l-1}t^j/j!$，$μ$为非负Radon测度；在反应参数的适当条件下，次临界范围$1<p<Q$中在极大位势条件下处理任意开集，临界情形$p=Q$中在总质量较小时处理有界区域，通过Wolff位势进行逐点估计。

Result: 在次临界范围$1<p<Q$（极大位势条件）与临界情形$p=Q$（小总质量条件）下，获得了非负解的存在性以及逐点Wolff位势上界。

Conclusion: 结果将Wolff位势刻画与存在性理论推广到Carnot群上带指数型反应项与测度数据的$p$-次Laplace方程，区分次临界与临界情形给出相应条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish existence and pointwise Wolff potential bounds for nonnegative solutions of $-Δ_{\mathbb{G},p}u=H_l(αu^β)+μ$ on Carnot groups, where $H_l(t)=e^t-\sum_{j=0}^{l-1}t^j/j!$ and $μ$ is a nonnegative Radon measure. Under suitable conditions on the reaction parameters, we treat arbitrary open sets in the subcritical range $1<p<Q$ under a maximal potential condition, and bounded domains in the critical case $p=Q$ under a small total mass condition.

</details>

Conclusion: 在较一般的非紧Riemann流形上，借助体积增长条件可获得该非线性不等式的Liouville型结果，且所得结果在Euclidean情形下具有新意与推广性，并通过实例说明其在部分情形下的最优性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the inequality $Δ_pu+Δ_qu+u^s|\nabla u|^t\leq 0$ on geodesically complete noncompact Riemannian manifolds. By a test function argument, we establish Liouville-type theorems under the upper bound of volume of geodesic ball. In the Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^n$, we obtain new nonexistence results which extend the result of Bhakta-Biswas-Filippucci \cite{BBF}. In particular, we have addressed the influence of the higher order term for $s<0$. At last, we present some examples to illustrate sharpness in some cases.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the existence and far-field behavior of an axisymmetric solution $(ρ,\mathbf{u},p)$ to the steady Euler system in an infinitely long cylindrical nozzle with a perturbed wall $r=R(x_1)$, containing a contact discontinuity $r=g_D(x_1)$. The background flow carries a nonzero swirl component $β_0\mathbf{e}_θ$, where $β_0$ is not necessarily small. Consequently, the background entropy, Bernoulli function, and angular momentum density depend on the radial variable, and their radial derivatives are not necessarily small.

### [90] [Inverse coefficient problem for the wave equation with nonlocal attenuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19854)
*Yavar Kian,Zouhour Rezig,Gunther Uhlmann*

Main category: math.AP

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像和粘弹性应用中自然出现带非局部时间衰减的波动方程，需要通过边界测量刻画介质性质并恢复其系数。

Method: 结合微分几何、含非局部项偏微分方程理论、复分析以及非局部算子理论，利用非局部衰减诱导的解的记忆性质进行分析。

Result: 可从任意短时间区间内的边界测量恢复一般类低阶系数；并证明可用支撑在不交时间区间上的测量恢复时间相关系数。

Conclusion: 在标准假设下，非局部时间衰减使经典波动方程中通常不可行的数据限制变得可行。

Abstract: In this article, we investigate the inverse problem of determining time-dependent first- and zeroth-order coefficients for a wave equation with nonlocal-in-time attenuation on a Riemannian manifold, using boundary measurements. This problem is motivated by applications in imaging and viscoelasticity, where such equations arise naturally, and we aim to characterize the properties of the underlying medium by recovering these coefficients. Our main objective is to exploit the memory properties of solutions induced by nonlocal attenuation, establishing the recovery of a general class of lower-order coefficients from boundary measurements collected over an arbitrarily small time interval. As a byproduct of our analysis, we also prove recovery results for time-dependent coefficients using measurements supported on disjoint time intervals. While such data restrictions are unavailable, and generally impossible, for the classical wave equation, we demonstrate that they become feasible under standard assumptions due to the presence of nonlocal-in-time attenuation. Our analysis combines techniques from differential geometry, the theory of partial differential equations with nonlocal terms, complex analysis, and the theory of nonlocal operators.

</details>


### [91] [Rectifiability of every finite order for stationary integral varifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19979)
*Sławomir Kolasiński*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brena、De Lellis与Franceschini证明了稳定整varifold支持集具有高阶正则性，但原证明方法不同。本文旨在提供一条独立且方法不同的替代证明路径。

Method: 在每个尺度及$\mathscr{H}^d$几乎处处的点上，将varifold与某个光滑化面积被积函数的Euler–Lagrange系统解的图像作比较，从而导出比尺度的任意幂次更快的excess-decay估计；再结合Santilli的高阶可整性刻画与Whitney延拓定理得出结论。

Result: 得到了比尺度的任意幂次更快的excess-decay估计，并由此推出支持集对每个正整数$k$与$0<\alpha<1$都是$(k,\alpha)$可整的，特别地是$\mathscr{C}^\infty$的。

Conclusion: 该工作独立于原证明，通过不同的分析手段重新得到了稳定整varifold支持集的最高阶光滑性结论。

Abstract: We give an alternative proof of a theorem of Brena, De Lellis and Franceschini (arXiv:2503.00649): the support of a stationary integral $d$ dimensional varifold in an open subset of $\mathbf{R}^n$ is $(\mathscr{H}^d, d)$ rectifiable of class $(k, α)$ for every positive integer $k$ and every $0 < α< 1$, and in particular of class $\mathscr{C}^{\infty}$. Our proof is independent of theirs and proceeds by different means. It compares the varifold, at every scale and at $\mathscr{H}^d$ almost every point, with the graph of a solution of the Euler--Lagrange system of a smoothed area integrand, and thereby derives an excess-decay estimate that is faster than any power of the scale; the conclusion then follows from Santilli's characterisation of higher order rectifiability together with Whitney's extension theorem.

</details>


### [92] [Non-simple blow-up for the Chern--Simons--Higgs equation: A priori analysis and constructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20042)
*Youngae Lee,Lei Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了自对偶 Chern–Simons–Higgs 方程非简单爆破解的存在性，覆盖此前未解决的有限高度情形，并刻画了非简单团的等质量、共同剖面、正多边形排列及质量间隙判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对偶 Chern–Simons–Higgs 方程的爆破具有丰富的多尺度结构，但既有理论主要局限于"简单"情形，即单个整体剖面解析每个集中点。非简单爆破在解析上更精细：多个相互作用的规则核需在各自尺度上同时被解析。该困难使此类解的存在性长期悬而未决。

Method: 针对一般有限高度团的定量剖面与矩估计，用于指导近似解的设计并厘清有限高度构造中的核间相互作用；另在单位圆盘上构造具有变化 Dirichlet 迹序列的两个平均场核。

Result: 证明首个非简单爆破解存在性结果，特别涵盖有限高度 Chern–Simons 情形；证明非简单团具有相等的极限核质量、共同的剖面类型与正多边形排列；确定其可能的总质量，并导出质量间隙判据以排除某些涡旋构型下的爆破。

Conclusion: 非简单爆破解的存在性得以建立，其团结构呈现出等核质量、共同剖面与规则几何排列等刚性规律；质量间隙判据可排除特定涡旋构型的爆破。单位圆盘构造进一步展示了沿变化 Dirichlet 迹序列的双平均场核现象。

Abstract: Blow-up in the self-dual Chern--Simons--Higgs equation has a rich multiscale structure, but the detailed theory has largely focused on the simple regime, where one entire profile resolves each concentration point. By contrast, non-simple blow-up is analytically more delicate: several interacting regular cores must be resolved simultaneously as they collapse toward a single vortex while remaining separated on their own scales. Because of this difficulty, the existence of such solutions has remained a long-standing open problem. To the best of our knowledge, we prove the first existence result for non-simple blow-up solutions of this equation, covering in particular the previously unresolved finite-height Chern--Simons regime.
  We also show that non-simple clusters have equal limiting core masses, a common profile type, and regular-polygon arrangements. We determine their possible total masses and derive a mass-gap criterion that rules out such blow-up for certain vortex configurations. Our quantitative profile and moment estimates for general finite-height clusters guide the design of the approximate solution and clarify the core interactions underlying the finite-height construction. A separate construction on the unit disk produces two mean-field cores along a sequence with varying Dirichlet traces.

</details>


### [93] [The Weighted Dual Minkowski Problem Under Group Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20047)
*Karoly J. Boroczky,Stephanie Mui,Gaoyong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究凸体加权q次对偶曲率测度的加权对偶Minkowski问题，在G-不变框架下给出q∈(0,1]的完整刻画、q∈(1,n)解的存在充要条件、q>1的充分条件，并推广了Henk-Pollehn的对偶曲率测度集中性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权对偶Minkowski问题由Huang-Lutwak-Yang-Zhang提出，此前关于对偶Minkowski问题解的存在性结果主要针对原点对称凸体等特殊情形。本文旨在将这些结果推广到更一般的G-不变设定，系统刻画加权对偶曲率测度并给出相应Minkowski问题的可解性条件。

Method: 在O(n)的闭子群G作用下，考虑G-不变凸体及其加权q次对偶曲率测度，通过凸几何分析与变分方法，分参数区间q∈(0,1]、q∈(1,n)及q>1讨论测度的刻画与解的存在性，并研究对偶曲率测度的集中现象。

Result: (i) 对q∈(0,1]给出G-不变加权对偶曲率测度的完整刻画；(ii) 对q∈(1,n)给出G-不变加权对偶Minkowski问题解存在的充要条件；(iii) 对q>1给出加权对偶曲率测度的充分条件；(iv) 推广了Henk-Pollehn的对偶曲率测度集中性质。

Conclusion: 本文在G-不变设定下建立了加权对偶Minkowski问题的较完整理论，推广了原点对称凸体上对偶Minkowski问题解存在性的既有结果，并拓展了对偶曲率测度集中性质。

Abstract: The paper studies the weighted dual Minkowski problem for the weighted $q$th dual curvature measure
  of convex bodies in $\mathbb R^n$ first posed by Huang, Lutwak, Yang, and Zhang.
  Most of the results are in the G-invariant setting,
  i.e. when the convex bodies are invariant under a closed subgroup $G$ of $O(n)$.
  They generalize previous results on the existence of solutions to the dual Minkowski problem
  for origin-symmetric convex bodies.
  It is proved:
  (i) a full characterization of the G-invariant weighted dual curvature measure for q\in(0,1],
  (ii) a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of solutions to the G-invariant weighted
  dual Minkowski problem for q\in(1,n),
  (iii) a sufficient condition for the G-invariant weighted dual curvature measure for q>1, and
  (iv) an extension of Henk-Pollehn's dual curvature measure concentration property.

</details>


### [94] [Multidimensional fractional material derivative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20060)
*Hubert Woszczek*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究多维分数阶物质导数及其对应的积分算子，并求解由该算子驱动的线性偏微分方程，证明测度解的存在唯一性、质量守恒条件及非负性与弱收敛条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多维Lévy行走的标度极限可由含多维分数阶物质导数的确定性方程描述，但该算子及其方程的解理论尚不完善，需要建立相应的算子性质与适定性理论。

Method: 分析多维分数阶物质导数的逐点表示，定义并刻画分数阶物质积分算子；结合Fourier-Laplace变换技术与直接卷积核构造，研究一类线性偏微分方程的测度解。

Result: 给出了分数阶物质导数与积分的逐点表示；对带概率测度积分速度向量和分布源项的方程，证明了测度数据下指数有界测度解的存在唯一性；得到了单位质量守恒的充要条件，以及非负性和弱收敛到δ₀的充分条件。

Conclusion: 建立了多维分数阶物质微积分算子的表示理论及其驱动方程的测度解框架，为多维Lévy行走标度极限的确定性描述提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: We analyze a nonlocal operator in space and time, called the multidimensional fractional material derivative. We derive its pointwise representation, which allows us to study its other properties. We define an inverse operator, called fractional material integral, and derive its pointwise representation. Furthermore, we analyze a class of linear partial differential equations, which corresponds to deterministic descriptions of the scaling limits of multidimensional Lévy walks, in which transport is driven by a multidimensional fractional material derivative with a speed vector integrated with respect to a suitable probability measure and a distributional source term. Using Fourier-Laplace transform techniques and a direct convolution-kernel construction, we prove the existence and uniqueness of exponentially bounded measure solutions for measure data. Moreover, we identify a necessary and sufficient condition on the source term for conservation of unit mass and provide separate sufficient conditions for non-negativity and weak convergence to $δ_0$.

</details>


### [95] [Existence of a nonnegative bound state for a higher-order logarithmic Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20096)
*Xin Ou,Xingyong Zhang,Yanhong Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究高阶对数薛定谔方程非负束缚态的存在性，通过幂律逼近、自主动方程唯一性与能量间隙、轮廓分解及重心min-max构造，在结构性与能量条件下证得原方程存在非平凡非负束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方程同时含一阶与高阶对数项，导致变分结构非光滑、紧性缺失，既有方法难以直接处理，需建立新的逼近与紧性分析框架。

Method: 引入一族幂律近似以逼近对数极限并给出关于逼近指数的统一估计；在自主非线性满足结构性条件时，证明自主动方程非负轮廓的平移唯一性、正集连通性及双轮廓阈值以下的能量间隙；分析小振幅行为，揭示极大值原理与紧支集二分性；对约束Palais-Smale序列建立轮廓分解，并构造重心型min-max水平低于分裂阈值。

Result: 获得自主动方程平移意义下非负轮廓的唯一性、正集连通性与能量间隙；m=1时小振幅解具正性，高阶奇数次则落入紧支集情形；建立约束Palais-Smale序列的轮廓分解并构造低于分裂阈值的重心min-max水平，防止质量经多轮廓损失，从而可过渡到对数极限。

Conclusion: 在所述结构性与能量条件下，原高阶对数薛定谔方程存在非平凡非负束缚态。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the existence of nonnegative bound states for the higher-order logarithmic Schrödinger equation $$ -αΔu+V(x)u-βu\ln|u| = γu(\ln|u|)^m, \qquad u\in H^1(\mathbb R^N), $$ where $α,β,γ>0$, $m$ is an odd positive integer and $V$ is a positive bounded potential converging to a constant at infinity. The simultaneous presence of the first- and higher-order logarithmic terms leads to a nonsmooth variational structure and additional compactness difficulties. We introduce a family of power-law approximations and derive estimates that are uniform as the approximation exponent tends to the logarithmic limit. Under a structural condition on the autonomous nonlinearity, we obtain uniqueness, up to translations, of the nonnegative autonomous profile with connected positivity set and a corresponding energy gap below the two-profile threshold. The small-amplitude behavior exhibits a maximum-principle/compact-support dichotomy: the case $m=1$ yields positivity, whereas the higher odd orders fall into the compact-support regime for the autonomous profile. We then establish a profile decomposition for constrained Palais-Smale sequences and construct a barycenter-based min-max level below the splitting threshold. This prevents loss of mass through multiple profiles and allows us to pass to the logarithmic limit. Under the stated structural and energy conditions, we obtain a nontrivial nonnegative bound state of the original equation.

</details>


### [96] [Hölder gradient estimates for fractional $p$-caloric functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20097)
*Davide Giovagnoli,David Jesus,Luis Silvestre*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了分数阶 $p$-caloric 函数在 $p \in (2,2/(1-s))$ 范围内的内部 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 正则性估计，并由此推出时间方向正则性的改进，得到 $p \in [1/(1-s),2/(1-s))$ 时内部时间连续可微性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶 $p$-caloric 方程的正则性理论中，高可微性（尤其是 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 与时间方向连续可微性）的可达参数范围尚不完整，作者意在拓宽并刻画正则性成立的 $p$ 范围。

Method: 采用内部 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 正则性估计的解析方法（针对分数阶抛物型方程），并结合由空间正则性导出时间正则性改进的技巧。

Result: 对 $p \in (2,2/(1-s))$ 得到某 $\alpha>0$ 的内部 $C^{1,\alpha}$ 正则性估计；进一步获得时间正则性改进，即在 $p \in [1/(1-s),2/(1-s))$ 时达到内部时间连续可微。

Conclusion: 该工作提升了分数阶 $p$-caloric 函数的正则性结论，并明确了正则性随 $p$ 与 $s$ 变化的参数区间。

Abstract: We establish interior $C^{1,α}$ regularity estimates, for some $α> 0$, for fractional $p$-caloric functions when $p$ is in the range $p \in (2,2/(1-s))$. As a consequence, we deduce improved regularity in time, which yields interior continuous differentiability in time for $p \in [1/(1-s), 2/(1-s))$.

</details>


### [97] [Stability for the 2D Micropolar equations near Couette flow via Green's function method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20109)
*Jie Qi,Lei Yu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用格林函数方法研究了全空间中微极方程解的稳定性，提出"主因子提取法"处理变系数耦合方程组，获得微极系统格林函数的尖锐估计，并证明了Couette流作用下解的最优衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有格林函数分析多针对标量方程，而微极系统是一个变系数的耦合方程组，其格林函数估计存在本质困难，缺乏有效处理手段，因此需要发展新的分析工具来研究此类复杂耦合流体系统的稳定性。

Method: 采用格林函数方法，针对变系数耦合方程组提出"主因子提取法"（dominant factor extraction method）以克服耦合项带来的估计困难，并据此建立微极系统的尖锐格林函数估计，进而分析Couette流作用下解的稳定性与衰减性。

Result: 建立了微极系统的尖锐格林函数估计；证明当初始数据满足‖(m_0,ω_0)‖_{L^1∩L^∞}≤c_0μ^{3/4}（c_0为与粘性系数μ无关的小常数）时，解在Couette流作用下保持O(μ^{3/4})量级；同时得到一般L^p范数下的最优衰减估计。

Conclusion: 主因子提取法有效解决了变系数耦合系统的格林函数估计难题，不仅给出了微极方程解的最优稳定性与衰减结果，还为复杂耦合流体系统的稳定性分析提供了一个通用框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we use the Green's function method to study the stability of solutions to micropolar equations in the whole space. An essential difficulty in estimating the Green's function lies in treating a coupled system of equations with variable coefficients, which is completely different from previous Green's function analyses for scalar equations. We propose a dominant factor extraction method to overcome this difficulty. Based on sharp Green's function estimates for the micropolar system, we prove that if the initial data (m_0, ω_0) satisfy \|(m_0, ω_{0})\|_{L^1\cap L^{\infty}}\leq c_0μ^{3/4} for some small constant c_0 independent of the viscosity coefficient μ, then the corresponding solution remains of order O(μ^{3/4}) under the effect of the Couette flow. Meanwhile, we establish the optimal decay estimates of solutions in the general L^p norm. More importantly, the dominant factor extraction method developed in this work provides a general framework for the stability analysis of complex coupled fluid systems.

</details>


### [98] [Large-Time Behavior for One-Dimensional Planar Compressible Magnetohydrodynamic Equations on Unbounded Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20126)
*Jing Li,Ziyin Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一维平面可压缩MHD系统在无界域中全局强解的大时间行为，通过构造辅助椭圆方程得到磁场平方幅值与其空间平均之差的平方根时间界，进而证明比容与温度的时空一致上下界及解的渐近稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画一维平面可压缩磁流体力学（MHD）系统全局强解在无界域（全直线Cauchy问题与半直线初边值问题）中的大时间渐近行为，在常值正黏性、热导率正比于温度非负幂次的假设下，给出比容与温度的时空一致有界性及解随时间的渐近稳定性。

Method: 对全直线Cauchy问题及半直线（热绝缘边界，磁场取齐次Neumann或齐次Dirichlet条件）初边值问题，关键构造一个辅助椭圆方程，并由MHD系统能量密度方程导出新的估计；据此得到磁场平方幅值与其空间平均之差的平方根时间界，进而推得比容的时空一致上界与正下界，并建立温度在时间上一致的上、下界。

Result: 新的椭圆方程估计表明磁场平方幅值与其空间平均的偏差满足平方根时间界；由此证明比容在空间与时间上具有一致上界和正下界，温度亦具时间一致的上、下界；全局强解当时间趋于无穷时渐近稳定。

Conclusion: 在一维平面可压缩MHD系统中，借助辅助椭圆方程导出的新估计可有效控制磁场平方幅值的空间涨落，从而获得比容与温度的时空一致有界性，并证实全局强解的大时间渐近稳定性；该方法适用于全直线及半直线（热绝缘、齐次Neumann/Dirichlet磁场边界）等情形。

Abstract: The large time behavior of global strong solutions is investigated for the one-dimensional planar compressible MHD system in unbounded domains. Constant positive viscosity is adopted, and heat conductivity is taken proportional to a nonnegative power of temperature. The Cauchy problem on the whole real line and two initial-boundary value problems on the half-line are considered. For these half-line problems, the boundary is thermally insulated, and either homogeneous Neumann or homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions are imposed on the magnetic field. Through the crucial construction of an auxiliary elliptic equation, a new estimate is derived from the equation for the energy density of the MHD system.
