This estimate demonstrates that the deviation between the squared magnitude of the magnetic field and its spatial average admits a square-root-in-time bound, which in turn implies that the specific volume admits uniform upper and positive lower bounds in both space and time. Moreover, the uniform-in-time upper and lower bounds for the temperature are also established, and the global solution is shown to be asymptotically stable as time tends to infinity.

</details>


### [99] [On an inverse boundary problem in elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20134)
*Lu Chen,Yan Jiang,Hongyu Liu,Longyue Tao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究三维静态各向同性弹性中Lamé参数λ与μ的同时识别问题，在多种结构性条件下由Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射获得唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性参数同时识别通常需要系数实解析或∇μ足够小等较强假设，本文旨在弱化这些限制并明确仅约束μ的结构条件即可实现唯一性。

Method: 对光滑系数，在μ满足轴对称、乘积区域上的乘法或加法分离、或沿固定方向拟解析等条件下，利用全Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射建立唯一性；轴对称与分离情形下使用部分边界数据确定μ。

Result: 在全Dirichlet-to-Neumann映射下证明(λ,μ)唯一；轴对称与分离情形下部分边界数据足以确定μ；结果不要求∇μ小、不要求系数实解析，也不对λ施加相应结构条件。

Conclusion: 给出了较弱条件下Lamé参数同时识别的唯一性定理，扩展了弹性反问题的适用范围并减少了对数据与系数正则性的依赖。

Abstract: We study the simultaneous identification of the Lamé parameters $λ$ and $μ$ in three-dimensional static isotropic elasticity. For smooth coefficients, we establish uniqueness from the full Dirichlet-to-Neumann map when $μ$ is axisymmetric, admits a multiplicative or additive separation on a product domain, or is quasianalytic in one fixed direction. These results require neither smallness of $\nablaμ$ nor real analyticity of the coefficients, and impose no corresponding structural condition on $λ$. In the axisymmetric and separated cases, partial boundary data suffice to determine $μ$.

</details>


### [100] [Regularity of varifolds with bounded anisotropic first variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20459)
*Antonio De Rosa,Benjy Firester,Raphael Tsiamis*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 对满足Michael-Simon不等式与二次暴露条件各向异性被积函数的m-变流形，证明均曲率在L^p (p>m)时的ε-正则性定理，并推广Allard正则性定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Allard正则性理论推广到各向异性情形，并刻画不接近椭球范数的各向异性实例，需要建立相应的ε-正则性定理。

Method: 针对各向异性被积函数（满足Michael-Simon不等式及二次暴露条件），在足够平坦的密度为1的点附近，证明m-变流形可表示为C^{1,α}图，结合各向异性Michael-Simon不等式证明结果。

Result: 在任意余维下建立了大范围各向异性被积函数（含接近面积泛函者）的各向异性Allard正则性定理；构造出同时满足一致标量原子条件与Michael-Simon不等式但不接近任何椭球范数的新型各向异性，包括各维数余维下特定q范围的ℓ^q范数及一类新的轴对称各向异性。

Conclusion: 该工作将各向异性正则性理论扩展到一大类被积函数，并首次给出非椭球型各向异性实例，拓展了经典Allard定理的适用范围。

Abstract: We prove an $\varepsilon$-regularity theorem for $m$-varifolds with mean curvature in $L^p$, $p>m$, with respect to an anisotropic integrand satisfying a Michael-Simon inequality and the quadratic exposed condition: near sufficiently flat density-one points, such varifolds are representable as $C^{1,α}$ graphs. Combined with the recent proof of the anisotropic Michael-Simon inequality, this establishes an anisotropic Allard regularity theorem in arbitrary codimension for a large class of anisotropic integrands, including those close to the area functional. We also exhibit the first examples of anisotropies satisfying both the uniform scalar atomic condition and the Michael-Simon inequality, that are not close to any ellipsoidal norm. These include the $\ell^q$ norms in every dimension and codimension, for explicit ranges of $q$, and a new class of axisymmetric anisotropies.

</details>


### [101] [Sharp regularity results for eikonal equations in Banach spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20145)
*Trí Minh Lê,Sebastián Tapia-García*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究Banach空间（有限维与无限维）上eikonal方程 $\|Df\|\equiv 1$ 的Gateaux可微解，揭示底层Banach空间的几何性质如何决定解的正则性与仿射性，并在有限维情形下刻画梯度取值受限于凸体边界的可微函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粘性解一般仅有Lipschitz正则性，但可微解具有更强的结构性质。欧氏空间已有Caffarelli与Crandall的观察：可微解局部属 $C^{1,1}$，且在整个 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上必为仿射。作者希望将这些现象推广到Banach空间框架，探究空间几何结构对解性质的影响。

Method: 在有限维与无限维Banach空间中研究eikonal方程 $\|Df\|\equiv 1$ 的Gateaux可微解，通过分析底层Banach空间的几何性质（如范数的光滑性、凸性、可微结构等）来推导解的正则性与仿射性结论；有限维情形下将梯度像约束到凸体边界，进而刻画相应的可微函数。

Result: 结果表明Gateaux可微解的正则性与仿射性由底层Banach空间的几何决定：空间的几何结构直接影响解是否具有更强正则性以及是否必为仿射。在有限维中，给出了梯度像约束于凸体边界的可微函数的刻画。

Conclusion: eikonal方程可微解的结构性质本质上依赖于所在Banach空间的几何，欧氏空间中的经典结论是这一更一般框架的特例；所得结果也为研究梯度约束的几何问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: Viscosity solutions of eikonal equations need not enjoy regularity beyond Lipschitz continuity. In contrast, differentiable solutions exhibit stronger structural properties. In the Euclidean setting, building on observations of Caffarelli and Crandall (Comm. Partial Differential Equations $\textbf{35}$ (2010), 391--414), one finds that differentiable solutions of the eikonal equation $\| Df \| \equiv 1 \,\, \text{in } \mathcal{U} \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ are of class $C^{1, 1}_{\mathrm{loc}}(\mathcal U)$. Moreover, if $\mathcal{U} = \mathbb{R}^d$, then $f$ must be affine. This manuscript revisits these phenomena in the setting of (finite-- and infinite--dimensional) Banach spaces. Our results show how the geometry of the underlying Banach space determines the regularity and affineness of Gateaux--differentiable solutions of eikonal equations. As a geometric application in finite dimensions, we characterize differentiable functions whose gradient ranges are constrained to boundaries of convex bodies.

</details>


### [102] [The linear instability of Kasner spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20809)
*Oliver Petersen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在无对称性假设下证明了Kasner时空沿大爆炸方向的线性稳定性，并发现除显式有限维非衰减自相似解外均为衰减解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所有宁静（非振荡）的4维真空大爆炸时空从单个大爆炸观察者看都渐近于Kasner时空；由于BKL猜想中的振荡效应，它们预期高度不稳定，本文旨在无对称性假设下于线性层面完整刻画这一不稳定性。

Method: 引入Kasner时空上的准正规模（quasinormal modes）概念（类比稳态黑洞时空的准正规模），并进行线性化分析，不做对称性假设。

Result: 证明Kasner时空沿大爆炸方向的线性稳定性，其非衰减解构成一个显式有限维空间：增长最快的解是Taub显式Bianchi II解的线性化（描述Kasner跃迁），其余非衰减解为这一解的非均匀类比以及线性化Kasner度规。

Conclusion: 在线性层面完整描述了大爆炸奇点附近Kasner时空的不稳定性，且无需对称性假设；准正规模概念为研究此类宇宙学奇点提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove linear stability of Kasner spacetimes in the direction of the Big Bang, up to an explicit finite dimensional space of non-decaying self-similar solutions. The fastest growing solution is the linearization of Taub's explicit Bianchi II solution, describing a Kasner transition. All other non-decaying solutions are inhomogeneous analogues of this, and the linearized Kasner metric. The key novelty is a notion of quasinormal modes on Kasner spacetimes, similar to quasinormal modes on stationary black holes spacetimes.
  All quiescent (as opposed to oscillatory) vacuum Big Bang spacetimes of dimension 4 are asymptotic to a Kasner spacetime from the perspective of a single observer at the Big Bang. They are expected to be highly unstable due to the oscillations conjectured by Belinski, Khalatnikov and Lifschitz. This paper provides a complete description of this instability on a linear level without symmetry assumptions.

</details>


### [103] [Double-Phase Neumann Problems with Variable Growth on Metric Measure Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20375)
*Ritul Dutta,M. Ashraf Bhat,G Sankara Raju Kosuru*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究度量测度空间中带变指数的双相Neumann问题，在边界测度满足上余维数界及增长函数局部可比条件下，建立分数极大算子的模估计，证明迹算子存在有界、能量泛函强制性、极小元存在性、极小元集闭凸且极小弱上梯度唯一，并给出极小元对Neumann数据扰动的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双相（double-phase）变指数问题与非标准增长问题在材料力学、非牛顿流体等模型中具有重要应用，但已有研究多集中于欧氏空间且较少涉及一般度量测度空间与Neumann边界条件。本文旨在将变指数双相Neumann问题的适定性理论推广到边界测度仅满足上余维数界\(0\leθ<1\)的度量测度空间框架下。

Method: 在度量测度空间区域上研究变指数双相Neumann问题，边界测度满足上余维数\(θ\)界（\(0\leθ<1\)）。在双相增长函数满足局部可比性条件下，先建立分数极大算子的模估计（modular estimate），据此证明相关迹算子的存在性与有界性；随后在归一化双相Newtonian空间上验证能量泛函的强制性，并在自反性假设下证明极小元存在。

Result: 证明了迹算子的存在性与有界性；建立了能量泛函在归一化双相Newtonian空间上的强制性，并在自反条件下得到极小元的存在性。进一步证明极小元集合是闭且凸的，且任意两个极小元几乎处处具有相同的最小弱上梯度。最后证明了极小元在Neumann数据扰动下的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作将变指数双相Neumann问题的适定性、极小元结构（闭凸性与弱上梯度唯一性）及解的稳定性系统地推广到边界测度仅满足上余维数界的度量测度空间，为一般度量框架下非标准增长变分问题的研究提供了模估计与迹算子等分析工具。

Abstract: We study a variable-exponent double-phase Neumann problem on a domain in a metric measure space, where the boundary measure satisfies an upper codimension-\(θ\) bound for \(0\leθ<1\). Under a local comparability condition on the double-phase growth function, we establish a modular estimate for a fractional maximal operator and use it to prove the existence and boundedness of the associated trace operator. We then establish coercivity of the associated energy functional on the normalized double-phase Newtonian space and, under reflexivity, prove the existence of minimizers. We further show that the set of minimizers is closed and convex, and that any two minimizers have the same minimal weak upper gradient almost everywhere. Finally, we prove the stability of minimizers under perturbations of the Neumann data.

</details>


### [104] [Compactly supported real scalar potentials realizing the Hardy uncertainty endpoint for Schrödinger evolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20202)
*Xiao-Ming Fu,Tianyang Sun*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在一维Schrödinger方程临界Hardy高斯权重框架下，构造了第一个具有实值、纯标量、有界光滑且空间紧支撑势的非零端点光滑解，解决了Cassano-Fanelli提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在[0,1]上一维Schrödinger方程的临界Hardy高斯权重情形下，已知的非零标量例子依赖于复值势；Cassano与Fanelli指出实值标量端点例子的存在性尚属公开问题，他们仅通过引入磁势才得到实电势与实磁势的例子。本文旨在填补这一空白，给出纯标量实值势的端点解。

Method: 构造$\mathbb{R}\times[0,1]$上$i\partial_t u+\partial_x^2 u=Vu$的非零光滑解，使其满足加权$L^2$端点条件$e^{x^2/4}u(\cdot,0),e^{x^2/4}u(\cdot,1)\in L^2(\mathbb{R})$；势$V$取为有界、光滑、实值、纯标量，并在所有时刻支撑于一个固定紧空间区间。构造的核心是显式的有理尾（rational-tail）实现。论文结果由多智能体系统Eureka获得，随后经作者验证。

Result: 成功得到满足临界Hardy高斯权重端点条件的非零光滑解，其势为实值纯标量、有界光滑且具有固定紧空间支撑；并给出支撑该构造的显式有理尾实现。

Conclusion: 该紧支撑端点例子正面回答了Cassano-Fanelli关于实值标量端点例子存在性的公开问题，仅在纯标量实值势下即可实现，无需引入磁势；同时提供了构造背后的显式有理尾机制。

Abstract: At the critical Hardy Gaussian weight for the Schrödinger equation in one space dimension on $[0,1]$, the known nonzero scalar example in the weighted $L^2$ class carries a complex-valued potential. Cassano and Fanelli observed that the existence of a real-valued scalar endpoint example was open, and produced examples with real electric and magnetic potentials only after introducing a magnetic potential. We construct a nonzero smooth solution of $i\partial_t u+\partial_x^2 u=Vu$ on $\mathbb{R}\times[0,1]$ such that $e^{x^2/4}u(\cdot,0),e^{x^2/4}u(\cdot,1)\in L^2(\mathbb{R})$ and the potential is bounded, smooth, real-valued, and purely scalar and is supported in one fixed compact spatial interval for all times. This compact-support endpoint example is the main result. We also record the explicit rational-tail realization underlying the construction. The main results of this paper were obtained by the multi-agent system Eureka and have subsequently been verified by the authors.

</details>


### [105] [On a higher-integrability estimate for Laplacians of convex solutions to the Monge-Ampère equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20216)
*Diego Maldonado*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 针对 $W^{2,1}_{\rm loc}(Ω)\cap C^1(Ω)$ 中的某类严格凸函数建立最大值原理，并据此得到 $W^{2,1+\eps}_{\rm loc}(Ω)$ 正则性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格凸函数在低正则性（仅 $W^{2,1}_{\rm loc}$ 与 $C^1$）条件下提供最大值原理，并借此提升解的局部可积性。

Method: 对属于 $W^{2,1}_{\rm loc}(Ω)\cap C^1(Ω)$ 的严格凸函数证明最大值原理，并将该原理作为工具推导 $W^{2,1+\eps}_{\rm loc}(Ω)$ 估计。

Result: 建立了该类严格凸函数的最大值原理，并获得了 $W^{2,1+\eps}_{\rm loc}(Ω)$ 局部正则性估计。

Conclusion: 最大值原理是获取高阶局部 Sobolev 正则性（$W^{2,1+\eps}_{\rm loc}$）的有效途径。

Abstract: A maximum principle for certain strictly convex functions belonging to $W^{2,1}_{\rm loc}(Ω)\cap C^1(Ω)$ is established and then applied to obtain a $W^{2,1 +\eps}_{\rm loc}(Ω)$-estimate.

</details>


### [106] [Three solutions for a quasilinear inclusion systems driven by a nonstandard Laplacian operator in $\mathbb{R}^N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20342)
*Cuiling Liu,Xingyong Zhang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究无界域上Orlicz-Sobolev空间中一类非齐次拟线性包含系统，利用Wu-Zhou三临界点定理，在超线性且次临界增长条件下证明了三个弱解的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将非光滑临界点理论推广到具有非线性增长条件的非标准函数空间问题中，扩展临界点方法在Orlicz-Sobolev空间中的适用性。

Method: 采用Wu-Zhou新建立的三临界点定理，结合变分分析，构造与Orlicz-Sobolev空间结构及非线性增长特性相协调的能量泛函，并通过精细估计技术处理局部Lipschitz泛函。

Result: 在非线性项H满足超线性增长与次临界渐近行为的精确结构条件下，证明了该类非齐次拟线性包含系统存在三个不同的弱解。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓展了临界点方法在非标准函数空间中的应用范围，为无界域上Orlicz-Sobolev空间中的非齐次拟线性包含系统提供了新的存在性结果。

Abstract: This paper establishes the existence of three distinct weak solutions for a class of nonhomogeneous quasilinear inclusion systems, which are governed by locally Lipschitz functionals within Orlicz-Sobolev spaces over unbounded domains $\mathbb{R}^{N}$.By employing a new three critical points theorem established by Wu-Zhou in [29], we extend the nonsmooth critical point theory to handle scenarios with nonlinear growth conditions.Our analysis imposes a precise structural condition on the nonlinear term $H$, requiring it to exhibit superlinear growth while maintaining subcritical asymptotic behavior. This condition establishes a delicate balance between growth restrictions and functional analytic requirements. The novelties of this work include the construction of suitable energy functionals that are consistent with both the structure of Orlicz-Sobolev spaces and the characteristics of nonlinear growth. Through rigorous variational analysis and careful estimation techniques, we establish the existence of three weak solutions under these generalized conditions, thereby significantly expanding the applicability of critical point methods in nonstandard function spaces.

</details>


### [107] [A porous medium equation with dominating weighted absorption: three types of self-similar solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20397)
*Razvan Gabriel Iagar,Diana-Rodica Munteanu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对带主导空间非齐次吸收项的多孔介质方程 $\partial_tu=\Delta u^m-|x|^\sigma u^p$（$1<p<m$，$\sigma>0$）的自相似解进行了完整分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类方程的自相似解是刻画一般解大时间渐近行为的关键工具，而吸收项的空间非齐次性（$|x|^\sigma$）使解的结构更为复杂，需要系统分类。

Method: 采用自相似拟设 $u(x,t)=t^{-\alpha}f(|x|t^\beta)$，将原方程化为关于轮廓 $f$ 的常微分方程，并研究其在原点与无穷远处的渐近性质，借此分类所有可能的解。

Result: 所有轮廓在无穷远处满足 $\lim_{\xi\to\infty}\xi^{\sigma/(p-1)}f(\xi)=(p-1)^{-1/(p-1)}$；在原点附近解分三类：（i）唯一解满足 $f(0)>0$、$f'(0)=0$；（ii）唯一解具有死核，即 $f\equiv0$ 于 $[0,\xi_0]$；（iii）存在 $K^*\in(0,\infty)$，对任意 $K\in(0,K^*)$ 至少存在一个解满足 $\lim_{\xi\to0}\xi^{-(\sigma+2)/(m-p)}f(\xi)=K$。

Conclusion: 自相似解可按原点行为分为三种类型：正值正则解、死核解以及以幂律奇异逼近原点的连续族解；这些解将用于后续工作中分析一般解的大时间行为。

Abstract: Self-similar solutions to the porous medium equation with dominating spatially inhomogeneous absorption $$ \partial_tu=Δu^m-|x|^σu^p, \quad (x,t)\in\real^N\times(0,\infty), \quad N\geq1, $$ with exponents $1<p<m$ and $σ>0$, are classified. Looking for solutions in the form $$ u(x,t)=t^{-α}f(|x|t^β), \quad α=\frac{σ+2}{σ(m-1)+2(p-1)}, \quad β=\frac{m-p}{σ(m-1)+2(p-1)}, $$ it is shown that all their profiles satisfy the behavior at infinity given by $$ \lim\limits_{ξ\to\infty}ξ^{σ/(p-1)}f(ξ)=\left(\frac{1}{p-1}\right)^{1/(p-1)}, $$ but the solutions strongly differ with respect to their behavior near the origin: there exist a unique solution with $f(0)>0$, $f'(0)=0$, another unique solution such that $f$ presents a \emph{dead-core}; that is, $f\equiv0$ for $ξ\in[0,ξ_0]$ for some $ξ_0>0$, and, finally, there exists $K^*\in(0,\infty)$ such that, for any $K\in(0,K^*)$, there is at least a solution such that $$ \lim\limits_{ξ\to0}ξ^{-(σ+2)/(m-p)}f(ξ)=K. $$ The large time behavior of general solutions, making strong use of these three types of self-similar solutions, will be addressed in a companion work.

</details>


### [108] [Rigidity for Convex Entire Solutions to the $k$-Hessian Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20445)
*Xingchen Zhou,Ruixuan Zhu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了 R^n 中 σ_k 方程（右端为正常数）的凸 C^2 整体解必为二次多项式，且无需对解本身附加增长性假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: σ_k 方程（k-Hessian 型方程）的整体解刚性是几何分析与完全非线性偏微分方程中的核心问题。已知结果（如 k=n 的 Monge–Ampère 方程、k=2 等情形）通常需要在解的梯度或自身增长性上作额外限制才能排除非多项式解，因此自然的问题是：这些增长假设是否本质必要。

Method: 针对 3 ≤ k ≤ n-1，研究右端为正常数的 σ_k 方程的凸 C^2 整体解；在不对解本身作增长性假设的条件下，通过整体刚性论证证明该解只能是二次多项式。

Result: 对每个 3 ≤ k ≤ n-1，R^n 中 σ_k 方程（右端为正的常数）的任意凸 C^2 整体解都是二次多项式。

Conclusion: 得到了 σ_k 方程整体刚性在较宽 k 范围（3 ≤ k ≤ n-1）内的无增长假设版本，表明此前的增长性条件对这类方程并非必需。

Abstract: We prove that for each $3\le k\le n-1$ a convex $C^2$ entire solution of $σ_k$ equation in $\mathbb{R}^n$ with positive constant right hand side is a quadratic polynomial, without a growth assumption on the solution itself.

</details>


### [109] [Radial Symmetry and Strict Radial Decrease for Master Equations with Decreasing Radial Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20476)
*Yahui Niu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究主方程(∂_t-Δ)^s u = R(|x|)f(u)，在f'(0)<0（原点局部阻尼）情形下，证明具有一致空间衰减的正有界经典解关于x径向对称且严格径向递减，首次给出可容纳原点局部阻尼机制的主算子对称性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有主算子（master operator）对称性结果要求f(0)=0且f'(0)≥0（或f在原点附近非递减），此时移动平面比较不等式的符号由非局部扩散项控制。当f'(0)<0时，比较不等式中的线性项与非局部扩散同阶但符号相反，破坏了标准截断扰动论证所依赖的比较原理，因此需要新的方法。

Method: 将直接移动平面法（direct method of moving planes）与这一不利符号相协调，利用解u的空间衰减与R在径向变量上的严格单调递减之间的相互作用来克服比较原理失效的障碍。

Result: 证明尽管存在上述障碍，任何具有一致空间衰减的正有界经典解在每个时刻t关于x均径向对称且严格径向递减。

Conclusion: 这是首个能够容纳原点处局部阻尼机制的主算子对称性结果，拓展了主方程对称性理论的适用范围。

Abstract: We study the master equation $ (\partial_t -Δ)^{s} u(x,t) = R(|x|)f(u(x,t))\quad\mbox{in}\ \mathbb{R}^n\times\mathbb{R}, $ where $s\in(0,1)$, $R$ is positive and strictly decreasing in the radial variable, and $f$ is positive, locally Lipschitz, and satisfies $f'(0)<0$. Existing symmetry results for this operator require $f(0)=0$ with $f'(0)\ge 0$ (or, more generally, that $f$ be non-decreasing near the origin), because the sign of the moving-plane comparison inequality is then controlled by the nonlocal diffusion term. When $f'(0)<0$, the linear term in the comparison inequality appears at the same order as the nonlocal diffusion but with the opposite sign, destroying the comparison principle underlying the standard cut-off perturbation argument. We prove that, despite this obstruction, every positive bounded classical solution with uniform spatial decay is radially symmetric and strictly radially decreasing in $x$ for each $t$. The proof reconciles the direct method of moving planes with this adverse sign by exploiting the interplay between the spatial decay of $u$ and the strict radial monotonicity of $R$. This appears to be the first symmetry result for the master operator that accommodates a local damping mechanism at the origin.

</details>


### [110] [Large-Time Behavior towards Composite Waves of Degenerate Shock and Rarefaction Wave for Modified KdV-Burgers Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20591)
*Namhyun Eun,Sungho Han,Jeongho Kim*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非凸通量修正KdV-Burgers方程，证明粘性-色散复合波（退化Oleinik激波+稀疏波）的全局存在性与时间渐近稳定性，允许激波与稀疏波强度不小时仍成立，且估计关于色散强度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸通量导致黎曼问题由复合波构成；此前Huang-Wang-Zhang仅在纯粘性(κ=0)情形建立对应复合波稳定性，本文旨在推广到粘性-色散情形并放宽稀疏波强度限制。

Method: 在单调区域分析退化粘性-色散激波剖面的结构性质（导数双侧逐点界、趋近两端态的收敛速率，常数与色散系数无关）；结合Kang-Vasseur发展的带位移a-收缩方法，在小H^1扰动下处理复合波。

Result: 证明小H^1扰动下解全局存在并渐近趋于（时移后的）粘性-色散复合波；稳定性估计关于色散强度κ一致；激波强度δ_S与稀疏波强度δ_R无需小，仅需δ_R≤5/18 δ_S及κδ_S^2/μ^2满足激波单调性条件。

Conclusion: 将纯粘性复合波稳定性结果推广至粘性-色散设置，放宽稀疏波强度限制，并给出退化激波剖面结构性质这一关键工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the modified Korteweg-de Vries-Burgers (mKdVB) equation \[u_t + (u^3)_x = μu_{xx} - κu_{xxx}\] with $μ>0$ and $κ>0$. Since the flux is non-convex, the (inviscid) Riemann problem may be solved by a composite wave of a degenerate Oleinik shock and a rarefaction wave. We prove the global existence of solutions and the time-asymptotic stability of the corresponding viscous-dispersive composite wave, up to a time-dependent shift, under small $H^1$ perturbations. The shock strength $δ_S$ and the rarefaction strength $δ_R$ need not be small and are restricted only by the explicit ratio $δ_R\le\frac{5}{18}δ_S$ and the quantity $κδ_S^2/μ^2$ governing the monotonicity of the shock. The stability estimate is uniform with respect to the dispersion strength $κ$.
  As a key ingredient of the stability analysis, we establish structural properties of the degenerate viscous-dispersive shock profile in the monotone regime, including two-sided pointwise bounds on its derivative and the rates at which it converges to its two end states. These decay rates coincide with those of the corresponding purely viscous profile, with constants independent of the dispersion coefficient. In the absence of dispersion, namely, when $κ=0$, the time-asymptotic stability of the corresponding viscous composite wave was established by Huang, Wang and Zhang [26]. The present work extends their result to the viscous-dispersive setting and relaxes the restriction on the rarefaction strength. The proof relies on the method of $a$-contraction with shifts (for viscous conservation laws) developed by Kang and Vasseur [32,33,35].

</details>


### [111] [Localization properties of a free boundary problem for cell polarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20609)
*B. Niethammer,M. Röger,J. J. L. Velázquez*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究时变约束障碍问题中活性蛋白密度的局部化，证明小质量极限下任意正时间发生瞬时局部化，并刻画慢时间尺度上的集中过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该自由边界问题源自细胞极化模型，用于描述外部信号影响下活性蛋白在细胞膜上的分布，探究信号驱动下蛋白质量如何集中具有建模意义。

Method: 针对时变约束障碍问题，考虑小质量极限，分析自由边界演化，在适当的慢时间尺度上对集中过程进行精确刻画，并用若干外部信号例子说明。

Result: 在小质量极限下存在瞬时局部化：对任意正时间 t>0，活性蛋白质量集中到信号取最大值的集合中；并给出该集中过程在慢时间尺度上的精确表征。

Conclusion: 小质量导致蛋白在信号最大处瞬时集中，慢时间尺度刻画揭示了该局部化动力学，例子验证了结论。

Abstract: We investigate localization in a time-dependent contrained obstacle problem for the density of an active protein on a cell membrane. This free boundary problem has been derived in Logioti et al. [17] as a model for cell polarization under the influence of an external signal. We show that in the limit of small mass there is instantaneous localization, that is the mass of the active protein concentrates in the set where the signal is maximal for any positive time $t>0$. We give a precise a characterization of this concentration process on the appropriate slow time scale and illustrate this for a few examples of the external signal.

</details>


### [112] [Existence of Weak Solutions and Higher-Order Regularity for a Heat-Wave Fluid-Structure Interaction System on a Periodic Strip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20627)
*Mohammad Mahabubur Rahman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究周期槽域上热-波流体结构耦合系统的高阶Sobolev正则性，证明整体弱解存在及至H^3的正则性，并说明由于耦合PDE系统的复杂性，H^3以上正则性无法通过半群生成元或PDE切向-法向方法闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抛物-双曲耦合系统存在内在正则性不匹配，探究能否在任意大s下建立高阶H^s估计，明确该系统可达的最高正则性水平。

Method: 在周期槽域上研究热-波流体结构相互作用系统；建立整体弱解存在性并推导至H^3的Sobolev正则性；分析半群生成元方法D(A^k)（k∈N）及基于PDE的切向-法向恢复程序能否突破H^3。

Result: 建立了整体弱解存在性和至H^3的Sobolev正则性，为目前该系统已知的最高正则性；证明即便初值任意正则，半群生成元方法和高阶PDE切向-法向方法均无法在H^3以上获得闭合估计。

Conclusion: 耦合PDE系统的复杂性导致H^3以上高阶正则性估计无法闭合，H^3是该系统在当前方法框架下的正则性上界。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a heat-wave fluid-structure interaction system posed on a periodic strip geometry and investigate whether higher-order $H^s$-estimates for arbitrarily large values of $s$ can be established for the coupled system despite the inherent mismatch of parabolic and hyperbolic regularity. We establish global existence of weak solutions and Sobolev regularity up to the $H^3$-level, which, to the best of our knowledge, is the highest regularity currently established for this system. We further show that, even for arbitrarily regular initial data, neither the semigroup approach through higher-order generator domains $D(A^k)$ with $k\in\mathbb{N}$ nor the PDE-based tangential-normal recovery procedure yields closed estimates beyond $H^3$ due to the complexities of the coupled PDE system.

</details>


### [113] [Stochastic Two-Dimensional Anisotropic Magnetohydrodynamics System with Multiplicative Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20660)
*Mohammad Mahabubur Rahman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究环面$\mathbb T^2$上水平方向仅含速度耗散的随机二维各向异性磁流体力学系统，在乘性噪声下证明了全局鞅解与全局概率弱解的存在性及路径唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性磁流体力学系统中耗散仅作用于部分方向，导致能量估计与控制机制不完整，经典方法难以直接应用；引入乘性噪声后需同时处理随机分析与偏微分方程的退化性困难。

Method: 在$\mathbb T^2$上考虑速度耗散仅沿水平方向的随机二维各向异性MHD系统，采用乘性噪声框架，结合Galerkin逼近、紧性论证与随机分析工具构造解。

Result: 证明了全局鞅解和全局概率弱解的存在性，并进一步建立了路径唯一性。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，在此类耗散退化的随机各向异性MHD系统中，弱意义下的适定性仍然成立，为研究部分耗散随机流体模型提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the stochastic two-dimensional anisotropic magnetohydrodynamics system on $\mathbb T^2$ with velocity dissipation acting only in the horizontal direction. Under multiplicative noise, we prove the existence of global martingale solutions and global probabilistically weak solutions, together with pathwise uniqueness.

</details>


### [114] [Dense orbits for scale-invariant rotationally symmetric solutions of the 2D Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20674)
*Ibrahim Suleiman*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明2D Euler方程的尺度不变m重对称涡量存在向前轨道在弱*拓扑下稠密于S，表明Elgindi-Murray-Said的松弛结论中的正则性假设不可放松。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Elgindi-Murray-Said证明正则涡量解松弛到分段常值稳态，本文旨在探究该正则性假设能否放松。

Method: 从线性机制出发：将给定阶跃函数从选定时间反向演化并叠加，使单一线性轨道逼近可数稠密族；再证明该机制在非线性Euler耦合下仍成立，通过稳定性估计控制每阶段误差。

Result: 当m≥4时，存在2D Euler系统的向前轨道在弱*拓扑下稠密于S。

Conclusion: 正则性假设不可放松；每阶段误差可通过取足够远的下一逼近时间而任意小，从而无限迭代构造成功。

Abstract: Let $m \ge 4$. We prove that there is a forward orbit of the 2D Euler system for scale-invariant $m$-fold symmetric vorticities, namely \begin{equation*}
  \partial_tg + 2G\partial_θg = 0, \quad
  4G + \partial_{θθ}G = g, \end{equation*} which is dense in $S :=\{g \in L_{m,\mathrm{odd}}^\infty(\mathbb{S}^1): g\sin(mθ) \ge 0 \text{ and }\|g\|_{L^\infty} \le 1\}$ equipped with the topology of weak$^*$ convergence on $L^\infty(\mathbb{S}^1)$, where $L_{m,\mathrm{odd}}^\infty(\mathbb{S}^1)$ denotes the space of odd and $m$-fold symmetric functions in $L^\infty(\mathbb{S}^1)$. The system above was first derived by Elgindi and Jeong, who showed well-posedness in the $m$-fold symmetric class. In subsequent work by Elgindi, Murray and Said, it was shown that solutions with regulated vorticity relax to piece-wise constant steady states with finitely many jumps; our construction shows that this regularity assumption cannot be relaxed. The proof starts from a simple linear mechanism: by evolving prescribed step functions backwards from chosen times and superimposing the resulting data, one can arrange for a single linear orbit to approximate a countable dense family. We show that this mechanism persists for the nonlinear Euler coupling. The key stability estimates imply that the error introduced at each stage becomes arbitrarily small when the next approximation time is taken sufficiently far in the future, allowing the construction to be iterated indefinitely.

</details>


### [115] [Discontinuity of the Vlasov--Poisson Flow in $L_x^pL_v^\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20682)
*Ke Chen,In-Jee Jeong,Quoc-Hung Nguyen,Sangwook Tae*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明Vlasov–Poisson方程的解映射在零初值处不连续：在 $X_p=L_x^pL_v^\infty$ 空间中，无论是吸引还是排斥相互作用，当 $d\le 3$ 且 $1\le p<\infty$ 时，轨迹映射与固定时刻映射均在零点处不连续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画Vlasov–Poisson方程解映射的正则性/连续性，明确该方程在混合范数空间 $X_p=L_x^pL_v^\infty$ 中于零初值处不具连续性，从而揭示其适定性结构的局限。

Method: 构造反例：选取光滑、非负、有界（不超过1）、支撑于同一紧集内的初值族，将窄空间包置于格点上；利用自由输运使不同速度在每个空间点选取不同的包，并借助关于场的微小性估计说明非线性特征线接近自由输运的特征线。同时给出相空间 $L^q$ 范数随时间一致趋于零的结论。

Result: 对每个 $T>0$，解在 $L^\infty([0,T];X_p)$ 中的轨迹在零点处不连续；对每个足够小的固定 $t>0$，取值于 $X_p$ 的固定时刻映射在零点处也不连续。反例族的质量与初始 $X_p$ 范数趋于零，但在观测时刻的解范数至少为1。相空间 $L^q$ 范数对每个 $1\le q<\infty$ 随时间一致收敛到零。

Conclusion: Vlasov–Poisson方程的解映射在 $X_p=L_x^pL_v^\infty$（$1\le p<\infty$，$d\le 3$）的零初值处不连续，吸引与排斥情形均成立；这表明确有反例显示质量与初始范数趋零而观测时刻解范数不趋零，故该空间中的连续性不成立。

Abstract: We prove that solution maps of the Vlasov--Poisson equation are discontinuous at the zero initial datum in $X_p=L_x^pL_v^\infty$ for every $1\leq p<\infty$, in $\mathbb{R}^{d} \times \mathbb{R}^{d}$ with $d \le 3$, and for both attractive and repulsive interactions. For every $T>0$, the trajectory of the solution in $L^\infty([0,T];X_p)$ is discontinuous at zero, and for every sufficiently small fixed $t>0$, the fixed-time map with values in $X_p$ is also discontinuous at zero. The counterexamples are smooth, nonnegative, bounded by one, and supported in a common compact subset of phase space. Their mass and initial $X_p$ norm tend to zero, whereas their solution norm at the observation time is at least one. The construction places narrow spatial packets on a lattice. Free transport allows a different velocity to select a packet at each spatial point, while a smallness estimate on the field ensures that the nonlinear characteristics are close to that of the free transport. For the same families, the phase-space $L^q$ norms converge to zero uniformly in time for every $1\leq q<\infty$.

</details>


### [116] [Asymptotics of nonlocal nonlinear Robin energies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20692)
*Serena Dipierro,Giuseppe Spadaro,Enrico Valdinoci*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带Robin型非局部外部条件的非线性非局部泛函的自由极小化问题。证明对任意参数α>0极小元存在、唯一且具衰减性，考察极小元与能量对α的映射正则性，并给出α→∞与α→0⁺的渐近展开；α→0⁺情形依外力项是否具零质量分为两类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画含Robin型非局部外部条件的非线性非局部变分问题，系统理解参数α对极小元、能量及其渐近行为的影响，尤其是α→0⁺时能量可能发散的情形。

Method: 对依赖正参数α的非线性非局部泛函进行自由极小化分析；证明极小元存在唯一性与无穷远处衰减性质；研究映射α↦u_α和α↦E_α的正则性；推导α→+∞及α→0⁺的渐近展开，并按外力项是否具零质量分情形讨论。

Result: 对每个α>0，极小元存在、唯一并具适当衰减性；得到α↦u_α与α↦E_α的正则性；获得能量在α→+∞与α→0⁺的渐近展开；在α→0⁺时，一种情形极限能量有极小元且E_α收敛到其能量，另一种情形E_α发散到−∞。

Conclusion: 该工作为带Robin型非局部外部条件的非局部变分问题提供了存在唯一性、正则性与渐近分析框架，揭示了α→0⁺时因外力项质量是否为零导致的本质差异。

Abstract: We study the free minimization problem for a nonlinear, nonlocal functional associated with Robin-type nonlocal exterior conditions depending on a positive parameter $α$.
  We prove that, for every $α>0$, the minimizer exists, is unique, and satisfies suitable decay properties at infinity. We also investigate the regularity of the maps $α\mapsto u_α$ and $α\mapsto E_α$, where~$u_α$ denotes the minimizer and $E_α$ the corresponding energy.
  Finally, we derive asymptotic expansions of the energy as $α\to+\infty$ and as $α\to0^+$. The latter regime requires distinguishing between two cases, depending on whether the forcing term has zero mass. In one case, the limiting energy possesses a minimizer and $E_α$ converges to its energy, but in the other case $E_α$ diverges to $-\infty$.

</details>


### [117] [Existence of strong initial traces for stochastic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20735)
*Marko Erceg,Nikola Konatar,Kenneth Karlsen,Darko Mitrovic*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明了有界随机标量守恒律的动力学解存在唯一强初始迹，无需给定初值或非退化条件，核心证明为路径方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机标量守恒律中通常需要给定初值或对通量施加非退化条件，但该文旨在去掉这些限制，证明任意有界动力学解仍存在唯一的强初始迹。

Method: 固定实现后，在爆破极限中缩放随机项与动力学测度消失，从而应用Panov紧性论证；退化通量区间通过递归降维处理。需参数化随机-Fubini构造保证鞅恒等式在同一全概率集上成立，并利用确定性时间平均与滤流右连续性得到联合可测初始迹。

Result: 证明了每个有界动力学解存在唯一强初始迹；路径迹几乎必然和均方意义下沿本质时间局部强收敛，并得到联合可测初始迹。

Conclusion: 随机设定下初始迹的存在唯一性可在无初值和非退化条件下成立，路径论证结合随机-Fubini构造与可测性处理可克服随机性带来的额外困难。

Abstract: We prove existence and uniqueness of a strong initial trace for every bounded kinetic solution of a stochastic scalar conservation law, although no initial value is prescribed and no nondegeneracy condition is imposed on the flux. The core of the proof is pathwise. After fixing a realization, the rescaled stochastic terms and kinetic measure vanish in the blow-up limit, so Panov's compactness argument applies; degenerate flux intervals are handled by recursive dimension reduction. The stochastic setting creates several additional difficulties. The martingale identities must remain valid on one common full-probability set throughout the reductions, which requires a parameterized stochastic-Fubini construction. Moreover, the pathwise trace is not automatically measurable because its exceptional sets may depend on the realization. Deterministic time averages and right-continuity of the filtration yield a jointly measurable initial trace, with local strong convergence along essential times, both almost surely and in mean.

</details>


### [118] [Regularity for axisymmetric Navier-Stokes with an Euler length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20762)
*Peter Constantin,Mihaela Ignatova,Vlad Vicol*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明3D Navier-Stokes方程轴对称适当弱解在消失尺度ℓ(t)下的Type II点态界蕴含局部正则性，用涡度重标极限的循环与势涡度论证，且第二导数先验假设可由方位涡度的Hölder界及势涡度界替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究轴对称Navier-Stokes方程在临界尺度下的奇点形成机制，探索Type II点态界是否足以防止爆破，并弱化此前对二阶导数的先验假设。

Method: 采用Euler尺度ℓ(t)（非增、倍加、ℓ→0且(-t)/ℓ²→0），对重标涡度系统取古代极限（ancient limits），将CIV26的循环与势涡度论证移植过来，并用方位涡度的Hölder界及势涡度界替代∇²u的先验界。

Result: 在t∈(-1,0)上若|u(·,t)|≤Cℓ(t)/(-t)且|∇²u(·,t)|≤C/((-t)ℓ(t))，则解在t=0前局部正则；该结论涵盖ℓ(t)=(-t)^γ (0<γ<1/2)及抛物尺度的对数放大。

Conclusion: Type II点态界在Euler尺度下足以保证轴对称适当弱解的正则性；第二导数先验条件可被方位涡度的Hölder界和势涡度界替代，从而推广了此前CIV26的结果。

Abstract: We prove local regularity for axisymmetric suitable weak solutions of the 3D Navier-Stokes equations, which are smooth before the terminal time $t=0$, and satisfy Type~II pointwise bounds at a vanishing length scale $\ell(t)$. We say $\ell(t)$ is an Euler length if it is non-increasing, satisfies a doubling condition, and if $\ell(t)\to0$ and $(-t)/\ell(t)^2\to0$ as $t\to0^-$. This includes power laws $\ell(t)=(-t)^γ$ with $0<γ<1/2$, and logarithmic enlargements of the parabolic length. Our main result shows that local bounds of the type $|u(\cdot,t)| \leq C\ell(t)/(-t)$ and $|\nabla^2 u(\cdot,t)|\leq C/((-t)\ell(t))$ for all $t\in (-1,0)$ imply regularity. The proof adapts the circulation and potential-vorticity argument of our earlier paper~\cite{CIV26} to ancient limits obtained from the rescaled vorticity system by zooming in. We show that the second derivative a priori assumption may be replaced by a Hölder bound on the azimuthal vorticity and a corresponding bound on its potential vorticity.

</details>


### [119] [Positive normalized solutions for a singular regularized $p(x)$-Laplacian Dirichlet problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20778)
*Mustafa Avci*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究在给定模块约束下由正则化 p(x)-Laplacian 驱动的奇异 Dirichlet 问题，通过构造非奇异逼近泛函的约束极小化序列并取奇异极限，得到正归一化弱解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异 Dirichlet 问题在正则化框架下缺乏直接的变分刻画，尤其在变指数与模块约束并存时，约束乘子的存在性、奇异源的全局控制以及奇异极限的紧性均需处理。

Method: 对正则化能量建立增长与可微性估计，结合强制性、模块紧性与有符号约束变分的容许性，构造一族非奇异逼近泛函的非负约束极小元；利用一致的边界下屏障、相容权条件与变指数 Hardy 不等式，得到 Euler--Lagrange 方程及唯一约束乘子，并对奇异源进行全局对偶控制，针对零迹 Sobolev 检验函数取极限。

Result: 每个逼近极小元满足带唯一约束乘子的近似 Euler--Lagrange 方程；奇异极限给出状态子列强 Sobolev 收敛与乘子收敛，并保持正性与规定模块，所得极限对为正的归一化弱解。

Conclusion: 通过仅在反应项上移除正则化而保持梯度正则化固定，可在模块约束下严格实现奇异问题的变分极限，获得正归一化弱解。

Abstract: We study a singular Dirichlet problem driven by a regularized $p(x)$-Laplacian under a prescribed modular constraint. Using growth and differentiability estimates for the regularized energy, together with coercivity and modular compactness, we construct nonnegative constrained minimizers for a family of nonsingular approximating functionals. Under the admissibility of signed constrained variations, each minimizer satisfies an approximate Euler--Lagrange equation with a uniquely determined constraint multiplier. An explicit uniform boundary lower barrier and a compatible weight condition, combined with a variable exponent Hardy inequality, provide global dual control of the singular sources and convergence against every zero-trace Sobolev test function. For these approximate minimizing pairs, the singular-limit result gives subsequential strong Sobolev convergence of the states and convergence of their multipliers, while preserving positivity and the prescribed modular. The limiting pair is therefore a positive normalized weak solution of the singular problem, obtained by removing the reaction regularization while keeping the gradient regularization fixed.

</details>


### [120] [Regularity of asymptotically axisymmetric solutions to the 3D Navier-Stokes equations with analytic forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20803)
*Peter Constantin,Mihaela Ignatova,Vlad Vicol*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 在三维Navier–Stokes方程中，若外力为实解析且解满足各向异性Type II角平均界与坍缩核区精确轴对称两个性质，则该解在假定奇点处正则；因此原构造中的外力既不能在奇点附近恒为零，也不能在空间变量上局部一致于时间地实解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenAI近期宣称在$C^\infty$光滑外力下证明了三维Navier–Stokes方程有限时间奇点形成。该构造依赖两个关键性质：(i)解的角平均满足各向异性Type II界；(ii)解在坍缩核区精确轴对称。本文旨在考察：若把外力正则性提高到实解析，这些性质是否仍能容许奇点存在。这直接关系到该奇点构造的稳健性，以及外力正则性对奇点形成的限制。

Method: 在实解析外力假设下，假设解满足性质(i)与(ii)。借鉴作者先前工作及姊妹论文的思路，利用先验界给出的各向异性长度尺度在假定奇点处做放大（zoom-in）分析，得到古代（ancient）极限解；再通过其PDE演化所施加的额外刚性推出矛盾，从而证明正则性。

Result: 证明：满足性质(i)、(ii)且外力实解析的解在假定奇点处正则。推论：在原构造中，以及任何满足(i)、(ii)且外力在奇异时刻前$C^2$有界的构造中，外力都不能在奇点附近恒为零，也不能在空间变量上局部一致于时间地实解析。

Conclusion: 奇点形成对外力的解析性（乃至局部非退化性）具有刚性限制：实解析外力与所述Type II各向异性、精确轴对称结构不相容。该结果从正则性角度刻画了该奇点构造的必要条件。

Abstract: OpenAI~\cite{OpenAIManuscript} has recently announced a proof of finite time singularity formation for the 3D Navier-Stokes equations, in the presence of a $C^\infty$-smooth body force. The construction in~\cite{OpenAIManuscript} has a few key features, among which we single out: (i) the angular mean of the solution satisfies specific Type II bounds which are anisotropic; (ii) the solution is exactly axisymmetric in a collapsing core region. In this paper we consider solutions of the 3D Navier-Stokes equations in the presence of a real-analytic body force, and assume that these solutions satisfy properties (i) and (ii) above. We prove that such solutions are in fact regular at the putative singular point. As a consequence, in the construction of~\cite{OpenAIManuscript}, and in any construction with properties (i) and (ii) whose force remains bounded in $C^2$ up to the singular time, the force can neither vanish identically near the singular point, nor be real analytic in the space variables, locally uniformly in time. The main idea of the proof is inspired by our earlier work~\cite{CIV26} and the companion paper~\cite{CIVEulerLength}: zooming-in at the putative singularity using the anisotropic length scales provided by the a priori bounds, we arrive at ancient limits whose PDE evolution imposes additional rigidity.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [The Remaining $K_4$-Free Case in the Multipartite Clique Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19177)
*Yuuki Kasugai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了Lo、Treglown和Zhao在K4-free家族研究中遗留的最后一个情形f(n,7,4)，对任意n≥1给出精确值：除n≡4 (mod 7)且n≥11时为⌊30n/7⌋−1外，均等于⌊30n/7⌋。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lo、Treglown和Zhao对K4-free家族进行了系统研究，其中f(n,7,4)是唯一尚未确定的剩余情形，本文旨在填补这一空白，同时推进对平衡r部图最小度极值问题的理解。

Method: 方法分两步：首先将对应的三色极值δ(n,7,3)归约为一个7×3整数矩阵问题并精确求解；其次通过结构论证证明，任何最小度δ(G)>132n/31的平衡7部K4-free图必为三色可染的。

Result: 建立了f(n,7,4)的完整刻画：f(n,7,4)=⌊30n/7⌋，但当n≡4 (mod 7)且n≥11时该值为⌊30n/7⌋−1。中间结果是δ(n,7,3)的精确值及图结构的三色性阈值δ(G)>132n/31。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了K4-free家族平衡7部图最小度极值问题的最后情形，给出的f(n,7,4)公式精确且对所有n≥1成立，其中仅存在一个模7的例外类。

Abstract: For integers $n,r,t$ with $2\le t\le r-1$, let $f(n,r,t+1)$ denote the largest possible minimum degree of a balanced $r$-partite graph with parts of size $n$ and containing no copy of $K_{t+1}$. Lo, Treglown and Zhao identified $f(n,7,4)$ as the only remaining case in their treatment of the $K_4$-free family. I determine this function for every $n\ge1$. First, the corresponding three-colourable extremum $δ(n,7,3)$ is reduced to a $7\times3$ integer matrix problem and determined exactly. Second, a structural argument shows that every balanced $7$-partite $K_4$-free graph $G$ with $δ(G)>\frac{132}{31}n$ is three-colourable. Consequently, $f(n,7,4)=\lfloor30n/7\rfloor$ except when $n\equiv4\pmod7$ and $n\ge11$, where it is one less.

</details>


### [122] [Lean-Certified Infinite Counterexamples to Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19195)
*Cameron Beeley*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文否证了"Written on the Wall II"猜想194：构造含四个参数的无限反例族，其主二参子族满足猜想假设且取等号，却无Hamilton路径，并在Lean 4中完成参数化的机器验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 猜想194断言：任意n>1的连通简单图，若满足alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G)，则含Hamilton路径。作者旨在检验该猜想是否成立。已有反例或其修正尝试（如对最小度加下界）是否有效尚不明确，因此需要构造反例族以判定该猜想整体真假。

Method: 定义l_avg(G)为各顶点开邻域导出子图独立数的平均。构造一个四参数图族，并重点分析其主二参子族G(s,t)（s>=1, t>=3）。计算其顶点数、连通性、独立数、l_avg、最小度，并证明其不具可迹性（无Hamilton路径）。整个无限子族以通用量化定理形式在Lean 4中机器验证，而非仅验证单个图。

Result: 主二参子族对每个s>=1, t>=3有(s+1)t^2个顶点、独立数t+1、l_avg(G)=t、最小度s，且无Hamilton路径；同时满足alpha(G)=1+l_avg(G)，即猜想假设取等号。因此该子族为反例。特例(s,t)=(1,3)含18个顶点。Lean 4证书是参数化的，覆盖整个无限族。

Conclusion: 猜想194为假。由于反例的最小度可任意大（s任意），不存在任何固定的最小度下界能修复该猜想。参数化机器验证强化了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: For a finite simple graph G, let alpha(G) denote its independence number and let
  l_avg(G) = (1 / |V(G)|) sum_{v in V(G)} alpha(G[N_G(v)])
  be the average independence number of its open neighbourhoods. Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194 asserts that every simple connected graph on n > 1 vertices satisfying alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G) has a Hamiltonian path. We give a four-parameter family of counterexamples. Its principal two-parameter subfamily satisfies the proposed inequality with equality: for every pair of integers s >= 1 and t >= 3 it has (s + 1)t^2 vertices, independence number t + 1, l_avg(G) = t, and minimum degree s, but has no Hamiltonian path. This entire infinite subfamily is machine-checked in Lean 4: one universally quantified theorem certifies its order, connectivity, independence number, average neighbourhood independence, minimum degree, conjecture hypothesis, and failure of traceability. Thus no fixed lower bound on the minimum degree repairs the conjecture. The case (s,t) = (1,3) has 18 vertices, but the formal certificate is parametric rather than a verification of that one graph alone.

</details>


### [123] [A Strongly Aperiodic Monotile in Three Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19214)
*Ioannis Tsiokos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个有理多面体3-球Chair44(R44),它是由七个立方体组成的"椅子"形,其24个外露单位面带有微小方锥特征;论文证明该拼块可拼铺ℝ³,且每个拼铺无平移周期、对称群阶至多24,均为同手性并具有唯一的无限嵌套超块层级。证明以几何论证(特征迫使拼铺落在注册格上)结合穷尽有限枚举,关键有限测试经机器验证,并在Lean 4中对所有有限门进行核检查(每个native_decide定理有一个具名编译器钩子)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Socolar与Taylor曾提出:是否存在单个、单连通的三维原型块,仅凭形状即可迫使非周期性,且不允许弱非周期拼铺。已有的Schmitt-Conway-Danzer双棱柱和三维Socolar-Taylor块或允许螺旋运动,或存在一个周期性堆叠方向,均不满足这一更强要求。因此需要构造一个不含此类缺陷的三维非周期单调块。

Method: 设计有理多面体3-球Q(Chair44/R44):七立方体"椅子"形,24个外露单位面带有微小方锥特征。构造基于Six Birds emergence calculus(第3.3节)对非周期单调块现象的解读,关键在于一个经机器检验的有限测试——拼块自身的接触规则在粗化后仍然成立,使解码后的父拼铺只服从该块的规则。证明由书面几何论证(特征迫使拼铺落在注册格上)与穷尽有限枚举结合;配套census由两个独立实现复现,每个有限门在Lean 4中核检查(每个native_decide定理对应一个具名编译器钩子),书面几何引理与逻辑装配亦为Lean定理。

Result: Q可由全等副本拼铺ℝ³(允许反射);每个此类拼铺无平移周期,对称群阶至多24;每个拼铺均为同手性,并携带唯一的无限嵌套超块层级。定理在Lean 4中经核检查(模上述具名编译器钩子)。

Conclusion: 该工作构造出满足Socolar-Taylor强条件的单个单连通三维非周期单调块Chair44,克服了既有三维例子中螺旋运动或周期堆叠方向的缺陷;并通过机器验证的有限测试与Lean 4形式化,使主定理达到(模具名编译器钩子的)核检查级别,书面证明作为阐释保留。

Abstract: Socolar and Taylor asked for a single, simply connected three-dimensional prototile that forces nonperiodicity by shape alone, admitting no weakly nonperiodic tiling; the Schmitt-Conway-Danzer biprism and the three-dimensional Socolar-Taylor tile admit screw motions or a periodic stacking direction. We exhibit a rational polyhedral $3$-ball $Q$, which we call Chair44 (R44): a seven-cube chair whose $24$ exposed unit panels carry tiny square-pyramid features, and prove, as a proof submission, that $Q$ admits tilings of $\mathbb{R}^3$ by congruent copies, reflections allowed, and that every such tiling has no translational period and a symmetry group of order at most $24$; every tiling is homochiral and carries a unique infinite hierarchy of nested supertiles. The solid was designed to a reading of the aperiodic-monotile phenomenon reached with the Six Birds emergence calculus (Section 3.3), and the construction turns on a single finite test, checked by machine: the tile's own contact rule survives coarsening, so that the decoded parent tiling obeys the tile's rule and no other. The proof combines a written geometric argument, that the features force every tiling onto a registered lattice, with exhaustive finite enumerations; the companion census is replayed by two independent implementations, every finite gate is kernel-checked in Lean 4 (modulo a named compiler hook per native_decide theorem), and the written geometric lemmas and the logical assembly are Lean theorems as well, so that the theorem is kernel-checked modulo the named compiler hooks; the written proofs remain as exposition.

</details>


### [124] [From one generator to loop order on the three-cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19218)
*Jonathan Washburn,Milan Zlatanović*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维立方体 $Q_3$ 上基于闭合步道的单子，证明交换式聚合（边权和、转移计数）无法决定约化环路词，并给出最短反例长度 14，以及有限/无界记忆、占有约束、四元数时钟等多种结构性判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究在图上闭合步道中，交换性（abelian）聚合信息（如边权求和、转移计数）是否足以恢复非交换的约化环路词，以及不同记忆模型与约束的识别能力边界。

Method: 以 $Q_3$（$1$-骨架基本群为 $F_5$）的单子 $\mathcal W$ 为对象：通过 additive one-step reading 的分析将其因子化到 directed transition counts；构造具有相同转移计数但不同约化环路词的闭合步道；引入有限状态读数、无界栈读数、顶点势/占有约束，以及 declared quarter-turn quaternion clock 等工具，并推广至 $Q_n$。

Result: 每个 additive one-step reading 都因子化通过 directed transition counts；存在转移计数相同但约化环路词不同的闭合步道，故交换聚合不能决定约化环路词。约化环路词给出 proper 非交换识别商。找到 abelianization 平凡但二阶换位子信息非平凡的最短闭合步道，长度 14。两状态读数可分离 an order pair，但对任意 $k\geq1$ 存在无 $k$-状态读数可分离的 factorial pair；无界栈可恢复约化环路词。整数值顶点函数下的势读数在闭合步道上为零，占有绑定非矩形；占有型约束在历史类上矩形当且仅当占有向量在该类上常量。四元数时钟给出第二个 proper 序敏感商，与约化词商不可比。上述障碍与最小长度 14 在 $n\geq3$ 的所有超立方体 $Q_n$ 上仍成立。

Conclusion: 该工作系统区分了交换式转移计数与约化环路词所承载的非交换信息，给出了有限记忆与无界记忆的严格分离、占有约束矩形的充要条件，以及一个可与约化词商比较的四元数序敏感商；主要结论（交换不可恢复性与最小长度 14）具有跨维度的普遍性。

Abstract: We study the monoid $\mathcal W$ of based closed walks on the $1$-skeleton of the three-dimensional cube $Q_3$. The fundamental group of this graph is the free group $F_5$. We prove that every additive one-step reading, given by a sum of edge weights in an abelian group, factors through the directed transition counts, and we exhibit two closed walks with equal transition counts and different reduced loop words. Hence commutative aggregation does not determine the reduced loop word.
  The reduced loop word gives a proper noncommutative recognition quotient. We also determine the shortest closed walk with trivial abelianization but nontrivial degree-two commutator information. Its minimum length in the cube edge metric is $14$.
  We also obtain a separation between finite and unbounded memory. A two-state reading separates an order pair, while for every $k\geq1$, there is an explicit factorial pair which no $k$-state reading separates. An unbounded stack recovers the reduced loop word on every walk.
  For integer-valued functions on the vertex set, potential readings vanish on closed walks, while occupation binding is not rectangular. More generally, every occupation-based constraint is rectangular on a class of histories if and only if the occupation vector is constant on that class.
  Finally, a declared quarter-turn quaternion clock gives a second proper order-sensitive congruence, incomparable with the reduced-word quotient. The obstruction to commutative recovery, and the minimum $14$, remain valid on every hypercube $Q_n$, $n\geq3$.

</details>


### [125] [Intractable enumeration problems are like Russian nesting dolls: structural properties of monomer-dimer coverings on two-dimensional quadratic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19231)
*Yong Kong*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文给出二维正方格子上s个二聚体覆盖数的精确解显式公式，含s-1层嵌套求和，时间复杂度O(2^s)，并由双指数增长序列决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s个二聚体覆盖计数属于#P完全问题，通常被认为难以求解。作者希望揭示该问题的精确解结构并给出显式公式。

Method: 构造精确解的显式表达式，公式包含s-1层嵌套求和；解的取值由呈现双指数增长的一个序列显式确定。

Result: 得到覆盖计数的显式公式，对应算法时间复杂度为O(2^s)（指数级），解由双指数增长序列决定。

Conclusion: 尽管问题属于#P完全类，s个二聚体覆盖计数仍可获得显式精确解，其计算复杂度为O(2^s)，克服了通用难解性带来的障碍。

Abstract: Counting the number of coverings of $s$ dimers on two-dimensional quadratic lattices is considered as intractable and belongs to \#P-complete class. We reveal the structure of the exact solution to the problem and provide an explicit formula for it, which includes $s-1$ nesting sums. This results in an exponential time complexity of $O(2^s)$. The solution is explicitly determined by a sequence that exhibits double-exponential growth.

</details>


### [126] [Schrijver-Delsarte rigidity in association schemes and undecidability of quantum graph homomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20678)
*Lorenzo Ciardo,Iris Hebbeker,Gideo Joubert,Jana Kreiß,Antoine Mottet*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了由经典度量结合方案导出的图族所参数化的量子同态问题是RE-完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子同态问题的计算复杂性分类是量子信息与复杂性理论的交叉难题；经典度量结合方案（如Kneser图、Johnson/Grassmann/Hamming图）提供了丰富的图族，此前缺乏对其RE-完全性的系统刻画，尤其是非语境性（non-contextuality）量子多项式映射的证明工具尚不完善。

Method: 发展了一种谱方法来建立量子多项式映射的非语境性。该方法结合了对Roberson关于投影堆积数的界（以Schrijver theta表示）的等式分析，以及受Erdős-Ko-Rado理论启发的结构性论证。

Result: 证明了由经典度量结合方案导出的图族（包括Kneser图、q-Kneser图，以及Johnson图、Grassmann图和Hamming图的补图）所参数化的量子同态问题均为RE-完全。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子同态问题的RE-完全性扩展至一大类源自度量结合方案的图族，并提供了结合谱方法与极值集合论结构论证的新工具，推进了量子同态问题复杂性的理解。

Abstract: We prove RE-completeness of the quantum homomorphism problem parameterised by families of graphs derived from the classic metric association schemes. These include Kneser graphs, $q$-Kneser graphs, and the complements of Johnson, Grassmann, and Hamming graphs. Our proof develops a spectral method for establishing non-contextuality of quantum polymorphisms. It combines an equality analysis of Roberson's bound on the projective packing number in terms of Schrijver's theta with a structural argument inspired by Erdős-Ko-Rado theory.

</details>


### [127] [A Complete Proof of the Strong Conjecture about $F$-Irregular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19245)
*Tatiana Dovzhenok,Artem Filuta*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了关于F-不规则图的强猜想：对任意阶至少为三的连通图F，存在无穷多个F-不规则图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Chartrand等人（1987）的存在性猜想仅针对特定情形，且此前工作未完全解决连通图F下无穷多个F-不规则图的存在性问题，因此需要从根本上推广该猜想并给出完整证明。

Method: 作者通过证明Dovzhenok、Filuta和Chuhai（2024）提出的强猜想，即对所有阶至少为三的连通图F构造性地证明存在无穷多个F-不规则图。本文为其2024年2月原稿的授权英文翻译。

Result: 成功证明了强猜想，表明对于任意阶至少为三的连通图F，均存在无穷多个F-不规则图，从而根本性地推广了Chartrand等人的经典存在性猜想。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了F-不规则图的强猜想，从根本上推广了经典结果，并作为原稿的授权英文翻译公开发表。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is called $F$-irregular if all its vertices have distinct $F$-degrees, defined as the number of subgraphs of $G$ isomorphic to a given graph $F$ and containing the respective vertex. We prove the Strong Conjecture about $F$-irregular graphs (Dovzhenok, Filuta, and Chuhai, 2024), which states that for every connected graph $F$ of order at least three, there exist infinitely many $F$-irregular graphs. Fundamentally generalizing the classical existence conjecture by Chartrand et al. (1987), this work presents an authorized English translation of our original February 2024 manuscript, which was publicly presented in full at two scientific conferences the same year.

</details>


### [128] [Sumsets of Ahlfors--David regular sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19299)
*Fred Tyrrell*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了有限(α,M)-Ahlfors–David正则集的sumset下界估计：|A+A| ≥ |A|^{1+c(1-α)/log M}，并给出了[0,1]上正则集的相应结论，指数中1/log M的依赖阶是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Ahlfors–David正则集的加法结构（sumset增长）问题，刻画正则性参数α与M如何影响和集大小的下界，并确定最优的定量依赖关系。

Method: 结合多尺度熵分解（multiscale entropy decomposition）与加法组合学中的逆定理（inverse results from additive combinatorics），论证Ahlfors–David正则性在每个尺度上强制产生确定的熵增益。

Result: 存在绝对常数c>0，使得对任意有限(α,M)-Ahlfors–David正则集A⊆[N]（0≤α<1, M≥2）有|A+A| ≥ |A|^{1+c(1-α)/log M}；并证明了[0,1]上正则集的对应结论；且指数中1/log M的依赖为最优阶。

Conclusion: Ahlfors–David正则性通过各尺度的熵增益保证了sumset的定量扩张，所得估计的指数依赖1/log M达到了最优阶。

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that for every finite $(α,M)$-Ahlfors--David regular set $A\subseteq[N]$ with $0\leqα<1$ and $M\geq2$, we have the sumset estimate \[|A+A|\geq |A|^{1+c(1-α)/\log M}.\] We also prove a corresponding statement for Ahlfors--David regular sets in $[0,1]$. The proof combines a multiscale entropy decomposition with inverse results from additive combinatorics, showing that Ahlfors--David regularity forces a definite entropy gain at each scale. The dependence $1/\log M$ in the exponent is of optimal order.

</details>


### [129] [Posets of Hyper $(b,t)$-ary Partitions are Distributive Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19326)
*Elsa Frankel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究整数分拆在细化序下构成偏序集的结构，将Propp-McConville-Sagan关于hyperbinary分拆的分配性结果推广到hyper (b,t)-ary分拆，证明其偏序集为分配格，其join-irreducible元素构成fence偏序集的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Birkhoff与Ziegler曾批评整数分拆在细化序下的偏序集结构不规则。近期Propp、McConville、Sagan证明hyperbinary分拆的偏序集是分配的，且join-irreducible元素构成的偏序集同构于fence偏序集。作者希望将这一结果推广到更一般的hyper (b,t)-ary分拆情形。

Method: 定义hyper (b,t)-ary分拆：所有部分为某正整数b的幂，且重数限制为t≥b。在此定义下研究细化序偏序集的结构，并将其与fence偏序集的推广建立同构关系。

Result: 证明hyper (b,t)-ary分拆的偏序集是分配格，其join-irreducible元素构成的偏序集同构于fence偏序集的一类推广。

Conclusion: hyper (b,t)-ary分拆在细化序下具有良好的代数与序结构，是分配格，统一并推广了hyperbinary分拆的已知结果。

Abstract: Posets of integer partitions, ordered by refinement, were criticized for unruly structural behavior by both Birkhoff and Ziegler. However, recent work of Propp, McConville, and Sagan demonstrated that such posets of hyperbinary partitions are distributive, with posets of join irreducible elements isomorphic to the well-studied class of fence posets. We provide a generalization of this result for hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions, where all parts are powers of some positive integer $b$, and multiplicities are restricted to $t\ge b$. Then, we show that posets of hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions are indeed also distributive lattices.

</details>


### [130] [The phylogenetic rank of a graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19372)
*Franklin Ashworth,Oliver Clarke,Jeffrey Giansiracusa,Jackson Jones,Julio Quijas-Aceves,Yue Ren*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的Pachter-Sturmfels系统发育秩，提出贪心与精确算法并构建含6、7个顶点所有图的秩数据库，否证了若干猜想，给出子可加性与上界，并完成秩为1的完全分类及若干达到⌈n/2⌉的无限族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pachter-Sturmfels系统发育秩刻画等距嵌入图所需的最小度量树数目，此前缺乏高效计算手段，且其若干结构性猜想（遗传性、⌈n/2⌉上界、广义4点猜想）尚未被验证。

Method: 将边长均为1的图等距嵌入赋予上确界范数的度量树乘积中，定义系统发育秩；设计贪心算法与精确算法计算该秩；据此构建6、7个顶点全部图的秩数据库进行反例搜索与验证。

Result: 建立了Pachter-Sturmfels系统发育秩的贪心与精确算法及6、7顶点图数据库；给出反例说明该秩非遗传、不受⌈n/2⌉界限制、且违背Pachter-Sturmfels广义4点猜想；证明其在1-和与某些2-顶点和下子可加，且平凡上界为n-1。

Conclusion: 完成系统发育秩为1的图的完全分类，并构造若干系统发育秩等于⌈n/2⌉的无限图族，修正了原有关于遗传性与上界的认识。

Abstract: The Pachter-Sturmfels phylogenetic rank of a graph G is the minimal number of metric trees needed to embed G isometrically. Here, all edges of G are of length one and the product of metric trees is endowed with the supremum norm. We develop both a greedy and an exact algorithm for computing phylogenetic ranks. Using our algorithms, we construct a database of phylogenetic ranks which includes all graphs on 6 and 7 vertices. In particular, we exhibit examples disproving that the phylogenetic rank is hereditary, bounded by $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$, and a generalised 4-point conjecture by Pachter and Sturmfels. In addition, we show that the phylogenetic rank is subadditive under 1-sums and certain 2-vertex-sums, and that it is trivially upper bounded by n-1, where n denotes the number of vertices. We also provide a complete classification of graphs with phylogenetic rank 1 and construct several infinite families with phylogenetic rank $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$.

</details>


### [131] [Total-coloring of planar graphs with maximum degree 6 and without prescribed 4-cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19503)
*Enqiang Zhu,Yangyang Zhou,Jin Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了最大度为6的平面图的全染色问题，证明了不含某些特殊4-圈的最大度6平面图是7-全可染的，改进了已有结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全染色猜想（TCC）由Behzad和Vizing独立提出，断言每个简单图都存在(Δ(G)+2)-全染色。对于平面图，唯一未解决的情形是Δ(G)=6。已有结果证明最大度为6且不含4-圈的平面图是7-全可染的，本文旨在放宽这一限制条件，改进该结果。

Method: 通过限制4-圈的类型（排除某些特殊4-圈），对最大度为6的平面图进行结构分析与染色构造，证明其7-全可染性。

Result: 证明了任何最大度为6且不含某些特殊4-圈的平面图是7-全可染的，这一结果改进（弱化了）此前"不含4-圈"的条件。

Conclusion: 本文改进了一个已验证的结论，扩展了最大度6平面图7-全可染的适用范围，为进一步研究Δ(G)=6平面图的全染色猜想提供了新进展。

Abstract: The Total Coloring Conjecture (TCC) is a challenging unsolved problem posed by Behzad and Vizing independently, which states that every simple graph $G$ admits a ($Δ(G)$ +2)-total-coloring, where $Δ(G)$ denotes the maximum degree of $G$. This conjecture has been confirmed for graphs with $Δ(G)\leq 5$. However, for planar graphs, the only open case is $Δ(G)=6$. It was known that planar graphs with maximum degree 6 and without 4-cycles are 7-totally-colorable. In this paper, we improve this result by showing that any planar graph $G$ of maximum degree 6, which does not contain some special 4-cycles, is 7-totally-colorable.

</details>


### [132] [The smallest square tileable by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19536)
*George M. Georgiou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了：27 是能用两两不可比较的整数矩形铺砌的最小正方形边长，即对所有 n≤26 及任意 k≥2，n×n 正方形均不存在这样的铺砌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croft、Falconer 与 Guy 在《Unsolved Problems in Geometry》问题 C5 中给出了 27×27 正方形由八个两两不可比较的整数矩形铺砌的构造，并提出 27 是否为最小边长（且不限制瓷砖数量）尚属未知。本文即解决这一公开问题。

Method: 证明结合两种结构性归约，将搜索空间缩减至 167 538 个候选瓷砖集，再对其做穷举搜索；搜索由两个独立编写的程序完成，且完整软件、构建说明与输出日志作为附件一并提供。

Result: 对每个整数 n≤26 和每个 k≥2，n×n 正方形都不存在由 k 个两两不可比较的整数矩形构成的铺砌，从而确认最小边长确为 27。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了问题 C5 中关于最小边长的问题，答案为正：27 是最小的可被两两不可比较整数矩形铺砌的正方形边长，且结论经由双程序独立验证并附带可复核的计算材料。

Abstract: Croft, Falconer and Guy ({Unsolved Problems in Geometry}, Problem~C5) exhibit a tiling of the $27\times27$ square by eight pairwise incomparable integer rectangles and remark that it is not known whether $27$ is the smallest side length of a square that can be tiled by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles, no restriction being placed on the number of tiles. We show that it is: for every integer $n\le26$ and every $k\ge2$, the $n\times n$ square admits no tiling by $k$ pairwise incomparable integer rectangles. The proof combines two structural reductions with an exhaustive search over the $167\,538$ surviving candidate tile sets, carried out by two independently written programs. The complete software, build instructions and output logs are included as ancillary files.

</details>


### [133] [Peeling sequences: a directional method for the three-block construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19540)
*Dániel Gábor Simon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有限平面点集的剥皮序列计数，证明 n 点集一般位置下最少剥皮序列数 g(n) 满足 g(n) ≤ g(S_n) = O(6.57^n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥皮序列（每次移除剩余点凸包顶点）的计数是组合几何中的经典问题，需要给出 g(n) 的更好上界，特别是改进已知的递归 3-分支构造 S_n 的分析。

Method: 提出一种通用方法用于证明递归 3-分支构造 S_n 的更优上界，结合方向限制（directional restrictions）与加权前缀树（weighted prefix-tree）论证。

Result: 证明 g(n) ≤ g(S_n) = O(6.57^n)。

Conclusion: 方向限制与加权前缀树方法可有效改进递归 3-分支构造的剥皮序列数目上界，给出 g(n) 的指数级上界 O(6.57^n)。

Abstract: A \emph{peeling sequence} of a finite planar point set is an ordering of point removals, in which
  each point is a vertex of the convex hull of the points not yet removed. Write $g(S)$ for the number of such sequences, and $g(n)$ for the minimum of $g(S)$ over $n$-point sets in general position. We present a method which can be used to prove better upper bounds on the previously analysed recursive 3-branch constructions $S_n$. In fact, we prove $g(n)\leq g(S_n)=O(6.57^n)$, using directional restrictions and a weighted prefix-tree argument.

</details>


### [134] [Ordered matchings versus triangles via pseudorandom triangle-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19632)
*Wen Chen,Qizhong Lin,Chunlin You*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究有序图的拉姆齐-图兰问题，证明随机有序匹配 $M$ 几乎必然满足 $\rt(K_3,M)=\Omega\!\left(n^{4/3}/(\log n)^{1/3}\right)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画有序图组合中单色有序子图必然出现的阈值。此前 Balko–Poljak 对随机匹配给出 $\Omega((n/\log n)^{5/4})$，而 Conlon–Fox–Lee–Sudakov 的一般存在性下界仅 $\Omega(n^{4/3}/\log n)$，两者之间相差一个 $\log n$ 因子，本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 结合三类工具：伪随机无三角形图构造、对保序嵌入的粗粒度编码，以及由 Brègman 不等式导出的排列避免估计。

Result: 对区间色数为 2 的 $n$ 顶点均匀随机有序匹配 $M$，几乎必然有 $\rt(K_3,M)=\Omega\!\left(n^{4/3}/(\log n)^{1/3}\right)$，较 Balko–Poljak 的下界更强，并比一般存在性下界改进一个 $\log n$ 因子。

Conclusion: 该结果提升了随机有序匹配下 $\rt(K_3,M)$ 的已知下界，表明 $\log n$ 的间隙可被消除，并展示了伪随机构造与排列避免方法在有序拉姆齐问题中的有效性。

Abstract: For ordered graphs $H_1,\ldots,H_t$, let $\rt(H_1,\ldots,H_t)$ denote the least integer $N$ such that every $t$-coloring of the edges of the naturally ordered complete graph on $[N]$ contains an ordered copy of $H_i$ in color $i$ for some $i\in[t]$. We prove that a uniformly random ordered matching $M$ on $n$ vertices with interval chromatic number two asymptotically almost surely satisfies \[
  \rt(K_3,M)
  =Ω\left(\frac{n^{4/3}}{(\log n)^{1/3}}\right). \] This strengthens the lower bound $Ω((n/\log n)^{5/4})$ of Balko and Poljak for such random matchings and improves the general existential lower bound of Conlon, Fox, Lee and Sudakov by a factor of $\log n$. The proof combines pseudorandom triangle-free graphs, a coarse encoding of order-preserving embeddings, and a permutation avoidance estimate derived from Brègman's inequality.

</details>


### [135] [Unimodality for IDP Lattice Simplices of Prime Normalized Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19637)
*Feihu Liu,Zihao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了具有整数分解性质且规范化体积为素数的格单纯形的 $h^*$-多项式是单峰的，并给出了若干单峰性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ferroni 构造了一族反例，推翻了 Ehrhart 理论中关于具有整数分解性质的格多面体的 $h^*$-多项式是单峰的这一著名猜想。这自然引出问题：当限制为格单纯形时，具有整数分解性质的格单纯形的 $h^*$-多项式是否仍然单峰。

Method: 通过研究具有整数分解性质（IDP）的格单纯形的 $h^*$-多项式，聚焦于规范化体积为素数这一特殊情形，并进一步分析并建立若干充分条件来判定其单峰性。

Result: 证明了每个具有整数分解性质且规范化体积为素数的格单纯形都具有单峰的 $h^*$-多项式；同时建立了此类单纯形 $h^*$-多项式单峰性的若干充分条件。

Conclusion: 尽管 Ferroni 的反例否定了格多面体层面的单峰性猜想，但在格单纯形的受限情形下，单峰性仍可在特定条件下（如规范化体积为素数）成立，并给出了更一般的充分条件。

Abstract: Recently, Ferroni constructed a family of counterexamples to the well-known conjecture in Ehrhart theory stating that the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice polytope with the integer decomposition property is unimodal. This raises the question of whether the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property remains unimodal. In this note, we prove that every lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property and prime normalized volume has a unimodal $h^*$-polynomial. Furthermore, we establish several sufficient conditions for the unimodality of the $h^*$-polynomial of such simplices.

</details>


### [136] [Maximum and Minimum Spectral Radii in an Exceptional Family for Edge-Disjoint Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19655)
*Xuanyu Cao,Chunxiang Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了连通图在给定最小度下，边不相交生成树数τ(G)≥k的尖锐邻接谱阈值，通过核心—placement参数化精确刻画例外族L²_H(n,k,δ)中邻接谱半径的极值与极值图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画τ(G)≥k的尖锐邻接谱阈值，需在Chang–Li–Zhang引入的例外族中最大化谱半径；此前该族中极值图的具体结构（尤其是Conjecture 2提出的匹配构型）尚不明确，因此需确定最大与最小谱半径及其极值图。

Method: 采用精确的核心—placement参数化表示，将缺失边图M作为有界核心的结构变量；结合统一的Schur补预解展开进行扰动分析，其中首个候选相关系数关于∑ d_M(x)²为仿射，首个placement敏感系数为平方负载泛函；并利用等 equitable 商矩阵精确计算极值谱半径。

Result: 对固定可行(k,δ)及充分大n，确定了L²_H(n,k,δ)中邻接谱半径的最大值与最小值。当h=δ−k≥2时，唯一最大化图（同构意义下）满足M≅K_{1,h}∪(h+1)K_1，例外边嵌套在大团一侧，表明Chang–Li–Zhang猜想2的匹配构型并非极值；对任意h≥1，唯一最小化图满足M≅hK_2∪2K_1，2h条例外边在大团一侧端点互异；并解决了h=0,1及全部等号情形。最大化图给出尖锐的全局邻接谱阈值。

Conclusion: 例外族中谱半径的极值由缺失边图M的结构决定：最大者为星形加孤立点构型而非匹配构型，最小者为匹配加两孤立点构型；由此修正并否定了原猜想中的匹配极值猜想，并得到τ(G)≥k的尖锐邻接谱阈值。

Abstract: For a connected graph $G$, let $τ(G)$ denote the maximum number of pairwise edge-disjoint spanning trees, and let $ρ(G)$ be its adjacency spectral radius. For integers $n\ge1$, $k\ge2$, and $k\leδ\le2k-1$, let $\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the class of connected $n$-vertex graphs with minimum degree $δ$, and let $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)\subseteq\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the exceptional family introduced by Chang, Li, and Zhang. For sufficiently large $n$, the sharp adjacency-spectral threshold for $τ(G)\ge k$ is determined by maximizing $ρ(G)$ over this family.
  Set $h=δ-k$. For each fixed admissible pair $(k,δ)$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the maximum and minimum adjacency spectral radii in $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)$. In the core--placement representation, let $M$ be the missing-edge graph of the bounded core. For $h\ge2$, the unique maximizer, up to isomorphism, satisfies $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$, with the exceptional edges nested on the large-clique side. Hence the matching configuration proposed in Conjecture~2 of Chang--Li--Zhang is not extremal. For every $h\ge1$, the unique minimizer satisfies $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$, with the $2h$ exceptional edges having distinct endpoints on the large-clique side. We also settle the cases $h=0,1$ and all equality cases.
  The proof combines an exact core--placement parametrization with a uniform Schur-complement resolvent expansion. The first candidate-dependent coefficient is affine in $\sum_{x\in V(M)}d_M(x)^2$, while the first placement-sensitive coefficient is a squared-load functional. Equitable quotient matrices yield the two extremal radii, and the maximizing graph gives the sharp global adjacency-spectral threshold.

</details>


### [137] [On integral polytopes related to Edmonds' problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19703)
*Hiroshi Hirai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将交换与非交换Edmonds问题统一为整多面体${\cal P}(A)$与${\cal Q}(A)$上的线性优化，建立了多面体层级并给出整性间隙下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换与非交换Edmonds问题是计算线性符号矩阵秩的核心问题，本文旨在用多面体视角统一刻画二者，并量化其间的整性间隙。

Method: 对线性符号矩阵$A=\sum_{k=1}^m A_k x_k$，取子行列式指数向量的凸包构造整多面体${\cal P}(A)$，并利用其blow-up $A^{\{d\}}=\sum_k A_k\otimes X_k$构造${\cal Q}(A)$，将问题转化为多面体上的线性优化；通过推广已知nc-rank结果建立层级${\cal P}(A)\subseteq{\cal P}^{\leq 2}(A)\subseteq\cdots={\cal Q}(A)$。

Result: 建立了整多面体层级${\cal P}(A)\subseteq{\cal P}^{\leq 2}(A)\subseteq{\cal P}^{\leq 3}(A)\subseteq\cdots={\cal Q}(A)$，证明${\cal Q}(A)$相对${\cal P}(A)$的整性间隙至少为$1/2$；当每个$A_k$为秩2反对称时，层级在第二层终止且间隙改进为$2/3$。

Conclusion: 多面体层级为交换与非交换Edmonds问题提供了统一框架；特殊结构（秩2反对称）可使层级有限终止并收紧整性间隙。

Abstract: In this paper, we study polyhedral aspects on commutative and noncommutative Edmonds' problems for computing the rank of linear symbolic matrix $A = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k x_k$. We regard them as linear optimization over integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A)$ and ${\cal Q}(A)$, respectively, which are obtained by the convex hulls of exponent vectors of subdeterminants of~$A$ and its blow-ups $A^{\{d\}} = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k \otimes X_k$ $(d=1,2,\ldots)$. By extending previously known results on nc-rank, we establish a hierarchy of integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 2}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 3}(A) \subseteq \cdots = {\cal Q}(A)$ and show that the integrality gap of ${\cal Q}(A)$ relative to ${\cal P}(A)$ is at least $1/2$. Further, we show that if each $A_k$ is rank-2 skew-symmetric, then the above hierarchy terminates at the second level and the integrality gap is improved to $2/3$.

</details>


### [138] [Paths maximize the expected range of graph-indexed random walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19728)
*Yinfeng Zhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在相同阶数的所有连通二部图中，路径图最大化（固定一顶点为0的）均匀随机整数同态的期望极差，从而建立BHM猜想的期望形式；并证明BHM不等式蕴含LNR不等式，进而得出LNR猜想为BHM的推论。证明结合了同态的限制与重缩放、零边收缩、秩的定量估计与归纳法，结果在Lean 4中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benjamini--Häggström--Mossel (BHM) 猜想与 Loebl--Nešetřil--Reed (LNR) 猜想是图论中关于随机同态极差的重要猜想，二者间的关系此前缺乏严格证明。本文旨在解决BHM猜想的期望形式，并厘清其与LNR猜想之间的蕴含关系。

Method: 对每个二部分类上的同态进行限制并重缩放，随后收缩使高度函数取常值的边（零边）；通过对这些零边秩的定量估计来补偿简单随机游走期望极差中的奇偶项，从而实现对顶点数的归纳。在此基础上，证明BHM不等式可推出任意连通图上均匀随机整数1-Lipschitz函数的LNR不等式。

Result: 证明了路径图在相同阶数连通二部图中最大化均匀随机整数同态的期望极差（固定一顶点为0），建立了BHM猜想的期望形式；并证明BHM不等式蕴含LNR不等式，因此LNR猜想成为BHM的推论。主要结果已在Lean 4中形式化并检验。

Conclusion: BHM猜想的期望形式得到证明，且其严格强于或蕴含LNR猜想，二者关系被完全确立。证明方法与形式化验证（Lean 4）提升了结果的可信度。

Abstract: We prove that a path maximizes the expected range of a uniformly chosen graph homomorphism into the integers, with one vertex pinned at zero, among all connected bipartite graphs of the same order. This establishes the expectation form of the Benjamini--Häggström--Mossel conjecture. The proof restricts and rescales a homomorphism on each bipartition class, then contracts the edges on which the resulting height function is constant. A quantitative estimate for the rank of these zero edges compensates for a parity term in the expected range of a simple random walk, allowing an induction on the number of vertices. We then prove that the BHM inequality implies the Loebl--Ne\v set\v ril--Reed inequality for uniformly chosen integer 1-Lipschitz functions on arbitrary connected graphs, and hence obtain the LNR conjecture as a corollary of BHM. The proof was obtained through interaction with OpenAI GPT-6 Astra and verified by the author. The main results have also been formalized and checked in Lean~4.

</details>


### [139] [On 2-distance-transitive circulant digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19734)
*Wei Jin,Cai Xia Li,Ping Shan Li,Xiao Lin Sun,Jue Wu,Fan Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了2-距离传递循环有向图，证明其必同构于若干特定图族之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环有向图是Cayley有向图中的重要类别，已有2-弧传递循环图的分类，但2-距离传递循环有向图的分类尚缺失。

Method: 在有限循环群上定义循环有向图，借助已有2-弧传递循环图的分类结果，对连通的2-距离传递循环有向图进行结构分析与同构分类。

Result: 主要定理表明每个连通2-距离传递循环有向图同构于：无向圈C_n、完全二部图K_{n/2,n/2}、m≥3,b≥2的完全多部图K_{m[b]}、奇数n/2时的K_{n/2,n/2}-(n/2)K_2、素数阶Paley图、有向圈\overrightarrow{C}_n、满足给定条件的定向图G(p^m,r)、r≥3,b≥2且rb=n的定向图C_r(b,1)，以及满足条件的字典积定向图G(p^m,r)[\overline{K}_d]。

Conclusion: 连通2-距离传递循环有向图被完全分类，统一涵盖无向图、有向图与定向图多类极值结构。

Abstract: Circulant digraphs form a prominent class of Cayley digraphs defined on finite cyclic groups. Building on the existing classification of $2$-arc-transitive circulant graphs, this paper presents a complete classification of $2$-distance-transitive circulant digraphs. Our main theorem establishes that every connected $2$-distance-transitive circulant digraph is isomorphic to one of the following: the undirected cycle $C_n$, the complete bipartite graph $\K_{\frac{n}{2},\frac{n}{2}}$, the complete multipartite graph $\K_{m[b]}$ with $m\geq 3,b\geq 2$, the graph $\K_{\frac{n}{2},\frac{n}{2}}-\frac{n}{2}\K_2$ for odd $\frac{n}{2}$, prime-order Paley graphs, the directed cycle $\overrightarrow{C}_n$, the oriented graph \(G(p^m,r)\) satisfying Condition~\ref{p-power-normal-2dt-cond}, the oriented graph \( C_r(b,1)\) with $r\geq 3,b\geq 2$ and $rb=n$, the lexicographic product oriented graph \( G(p^m,r)[\overline{\K}_d]\) where \(G(p^m,r)\) obeys Condition~\ref{p-power-normal-2dt-cond}.

</details>


### [140] [A complete classification of permutation binomials of the form $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20354)
*Xiang Fan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 分类了有限域 $\mathbb F_{q^e}$ 上形如 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 的置换二项式，给出其置换性的充要条件，证明 Masuda–Rubio–Santiago 猜想，并计数不同置换函数；进一步分类 $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ 在 $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$ 时的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上特定形式二项式 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 何时为置换多项式，并验证/证明已有猜想（该族置换二项式均来自 $(X^{q^h}+aX)\circ X^r$），同时推广到更一般形式并计数。

Method: 通过必要性论证：选取 Hermite 幂和，利用 Lucas 定理将系数转化为数位条件；借助 Farey 引导的局部论证逐步确定基 $q$ 数位，并由循环旋转得到逆同余。特征 $2$ 情形使用 mod $4$ 提升到辅助环以保留模 $2$ 丢失的端点信息。

Result: 对任意素数幂 $q$ 与 $e\ge2$，二项式 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 为置换当且仅当 $\gcd(r,q-1)=1$、$(-a)^{\ell_e(q)}\ne1$ 且存在与 $e$ 互素的 $1\le h<e$ 使 $r\ell_h(q)\equiv1\pmod{\ell_e(q)}$；并确定该族表示的不同置换函数的精确数目。

Conclusion: 完整分类了该族置换二项式，证明 Masuda–Rubio–Santiago 猜想，并推广到 $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ 在 $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$ 的更广情形。

Abstract: We classify, for every prime power $q$ and every $e\geqslant2$, the permutation binomials $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ over $\mathbb F_{q^e}$. Writing $\ell_j(q)=(q^j-1)/(q-1)$, such a binomial is a permutation if and only if $\gcd(r,q-1)=1$, $(-a)^{\ell_e(q)}\ne1$, and $r\ell_h(q)\equiv1\pmod{\ell_e(q)}$ for some $1\leqslant h<e$ coprime to $e$. This proves a conjecture of Masuda, Rubio, and Santiago: every permutation binomial of this form arises from $(X^{q^h}+aX)\circ X^r$ for a suitable $h$. We also determine the exact number of distinct permutation functions represented by this family. As a further consequence, we completely classify the broader family $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ in the coprime-index case $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$. The new ingredient in the main classification is the necessity argument: selected Hermite power sums are organized so that Lucas' theorem turns their coefficients into digit conditions; a Farey-guided local argument then forces successive base-$q$ digits, and cyclic rotations yield the inverse congruence. In characteristic $2$, a mod-$4$ lift to an auxiliary ring retains endpoint information lost modulo $2$.

</details>


### [141] [Spreads of degrees in graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19762)
*Yair Caro,Riste Škrekovski,Christina Zarb*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图论中的"spread"参数 sp(G,k)，首先对任意图给出了基于阶数 n、边数 m 和最小度 δ 的下界，统一并推广了 Caro–Lauri–Zarb 与 Caro–West 的结果；随后应用于极大外平面图，确定 MOP(n,2)=4n/9+O(1)，改进上界至 ⌈(4n+10)/9⌉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caro、Lauri 和 Zarb 研究了极大外平面图的 MOP(n,k)，对除 k=2 外的所有 k 都确定了其值（相差一个加性常数），但 k=2 的情形尚未解决，仅有 4n/9≤MOP(n,2)≤(5n+19)/11 的粗略界。本文旨在填补这一空白，并给出更一般的图论下界。

Method: 定义 spread sp(B) 为集合 B 中顶点度数的极差，sp(G,k) 为满足 sp(B)≤k 的最大集合基数。首先对任意图 G，利用其阶数 n、边数 m 与最小度 δ 推导 sp(G,k) 的下界，证明该界蕴含 Caro–Lauri–Zarb 的界以及 k=0 时 Caro–West 的 rep(G)≥⌈n/(2d−2δ+1)⌉（其中 d=2m/n）；刻画等号成立的条件，构造达到该界的显式图，并证明当 n≥n₀(δ,k,d) 时该界对所有图是精确的。然后针对极大外平面图调整计数，得到 MOP(n,2) 的精确估计。

Result: 对一般图，所给下界包含并推广了 Caro–Lauri–Zarb 与 Caro–West 的已有结果，且在大 n 时精确。应用于极大外平面图，证明对所有 n≥14 有 MOP(n,2)≥⌈(4n+10)/9⌉，并在 n≡2 (mod 18) 时取等号；进而得到 MOP(n,2)=4n/9+O(1) 对一切 n 成立，改进了原先的 (5n+19)/11 上界。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Caro–Lauri–Zarb 遗留的 k=2 情形，确定极大外平面图的 MOP(n,2)=4n/9+O(1)，并给出了一个适用于任意图的、在渐近意义下精确的 sp(G,k) 下界，统一了此前多个相关结果。

Abstract: For a graph $G$ and a set $B\subseteq V(G)$, the spread $\mathrm{sp}(B)$ of $B$ is the difference between the largest and the smallest degree in $G$ of a vertex of $B$, and for an integer $k\geq0$ the parameter $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ is the largest cardinality of a set $B$ with $\mathrm{sp}(B)\leq k$. Caro, Lauri and Zarb derived a lower bound for $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ and, among several families of graphs, considered \[ \mathrm{MOP}(n,k)=\min \{\mathrm{sp}(G,k):G\text{ is a maximal outerplanar graph of order }n\} \] and determined $\mathrm{MOP}(n,k)$ up to an additive constant for every $k\not =2,$ leaving the case $k=2$ open, with the bounds $4n/9\leq \mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\leq (5n+19)/11$. We first prove a lower bound on $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ for an arbitrary graph $G$ in terms of its order $n$, its number of edges $m$ and its minimum degree $δ$. This lower bound contains the bounds of Caro, Lauri and Zarb and, for $k=0$, the bound $\mathrm{rep}(G)\geq \left\lceil n/(2d-2δ+1)\right\rceil $ of Caro and West, where $d=2m/n$. We determine when this lower bound is attained, exhibit explicit graphs attaining it, and show that it is exact for all graphs once $n\geq n_{0}(δ,k,d)$. We then apply the bound to maximal outerplanar graphs: adjusting the count to this class we prove \[ \mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\geq \left\lceil \frac{4n+10}{9}\right\rceil \qquad \text{for every }n\geq 14, \] with equality for $n\equiv 2\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 18)$, and $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)=4n/9+O(1)$ for every $n$.

</details>


### [142] [Combinatorics of hyperplane arrangements and Witten zeta function at the origin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20740)
*Kam Cheong Au,Kazuhiro Onodera*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将超平面配置的组合学引入紧Lie群表示zeta函数研究，给出Witten zeta函数在0处及其导数的一般公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典解析方法难以获得Witten zeta函数在特殊点处的简洁公式，因此需要引入新的组合工具。

Method: 将根系统对应的Witten zeta函数与超平面配置的相交偏序集联系起来，利用其Möbius函数处理更广的一类锥形zeta函数。

Result: 用各类抛物子系统指数给出了ζ_Φ(0)与ζ_Φ'(0)的优雅公式，并将结果推广到锥形zeta函数。

Conclusion: 超平面配置的组合方法为表示zeta函数特殊值计算提供了有效新途径，所得公式难以由传统解析技术直接得到。

Abstract: We introduce a new method that brings the combinatorics of hyperplane arrangements into the study of representation zeta functions of compact Lie groups. For the Witten zeta function $ζ_Φ(s)$ associated with a root system $Φ$, our method yields elegant formulas for $ζ_Φ(0)$ and $ζ_Φ'(0)$ in terms of the exponents of various parabolic subsystems of $Φ$. Such formulas do not appear to be readily accessible through the conventional analytic techniques in the literature. More generally, the method applies to a broad family of conical zeta functions, expressing these two special values through the Möbius function of the intersection poset of the associated hyperplane arrangement.

</details>


### [143] [The resonance graphs of nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19766)
*Lingmei Liang,Heping Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出了判定纳米管与环面多联六边形（toroidal polyhex）共振图中两个完美匹配是否处于同一连通分量的充要条件：它们沿x轴（环面情形为经线与纬线）具有相同割流量且具有相同梯子结构；对环面多联六边形还借助环面上简单闭曲线的同伦类，并在两完美匹配含交错六边形时可用简单流简化判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作针对冠状体系（coronoid systems）与基本纳米管，利用跨割流量给出了两完美匹配位于共振图同一连通分量的判据，但该判据的充分性对一般纳米管与环面多联六边形（或富勒烯）不成立。本文旨在强化这一条件，为更一般的碳网络结构建立有效的连通性判据。

Method: 将冠状体系、纳米管与环面多联六边形统一视为由六边形连接碳原子构成的碳网络；利用跨割（across cuts）的流量刻画完美匹配的等价关系，并在纳米管情形引入沿x轴的割、在环面情形引入沿经线与纬线的割以及"梯子（ladders）"结构；对环面多联六边形进一步采用环面上简单闭曲线的同伦类进行分类，并讨论在两完美匹配具有交错六边形时仅用简单流即可简化的判据。

Result: 建立了纳米管与环面多联六边形共振图连通分量的判别准则：两个完美匹配处于同一连通分量，当且仅当它们沿x轴（纳米管）或沿经线与纬线（环面多联六边形）具有相同的割流量，并且具有相同的梯子结构。对环面多联六边形，借助同伦类给出了相应刻画，并证明在含交错六边形时可仅用简单流来简化判据。

Conclusion: 该工作把共振图连通性判据从冠状体系与基本纳米管推广到一般纳米管与环面多联六边形（富勒烯类），并通过同伦类与简单流方法简化了环面情形的判别条件，为碳网络的共振图结构分析提供了更普适的工具。

Abstract: Coronoid systems, nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes (or fullerenes) can all be regarded as carbon networks composed of carbon atoms linked in hexagonal shapes. The resonance graphs of coronoid systems and nanotubes are not necessarily connected. For coronoid systems and elementary nanotubes, by using flow across cuts the present authors gave criteria for two perfect matchings lying in the same connected component of the resonance graph (Discrete Appl. Math. 395 (2026) 443-455). However, the sufficiency of such criterion does not hold for general nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes. In this paper we strengthen this requirement to obtain valid criteria for two perfect matchings of a nanotube (resp. toroidal polyhex) to lie in the same connected component of its resonance graph: they have the same flows across cuts along the $x$-axis (resp. longitude and latitude) and the same ladders. For toroidal polyhexes, our method uses homotopic classes of simple loops on the torus, and the above criterion can be simplified by using only simple flows, for the case in which two perfect matchings have alternating hexagons.

</details>


### [144] [Nearly Spanning Regular Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19777)
*Varun Sivashankar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文肯定回答了Alon-Mubayi关于大型正则图中存在覆盖大部分顶点的k-正则子图的问题，并对一般k,r给出了ε=O_k(r^{-1/2})，对k=2及奇数r给出了最优的ε=1/(r^2-3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon与Mubayi提出问题：对任意正整数k与ε>0，是否存在r_0=r_0(k,ε)，使得每个r-正则图（r≥r_0，顶点数n）都含有一个k-正则子图覆盖至少(1-ε)n个顶点。此前该猜想仅在k∈{1,2}以及k与r均为偶数时被验证，一般情形仍待解决，本文旨在对这一开放问题给出肯定回答。

Method: 针对一般k和r，构造并证明存在覆盖至少(1-ε)n顶点的k-正则子图，取ε=O_k(r^{-1/2})；对k=2且奇数r≥3的特殊情形，进一步精细分析并证明ε=1/(r^2-3)即已足够。

Result: 对一般的k和r，证明ε=O_k(r^{-1/2})时结论成立；对k=2且每个奇数r≥3，证明ε=1/(r^2-3)足够，且该界是最优的（best possible）。

Conclusion: 本文肯定回答了Alon-Mubayi的问题，将已知结果从k∈{1,2}及k,r均为偶数的情形推广到一般k与r，并在k=2、奇数r时给出了最优的覆盖比例界。

Abstract: Alon and Mubayi asked whether, for every integer $k\ge1$ and every $\varepsilon>0$, there exists $r_0=r_0(k,\varepsilon)$ such that every $r$-regular graph on $n$ vertices with $r\ge r_0$ contains a $k$-regular subgraph covering at least $(1-\varepsilon)n$ vertices. Previously, the conjecture was known for $k\in\{1,2\}$ and for $k$ and $r$ both even. We answer this question affirmatively for general $k$ and $r$ with $\varepsilon=O_k(r^{-1/2})$. For $k=2$ and every odd $r\ge3$, we show that $\varepsilon=1/(r^2-3)$ suffices which is best possible.

</details>


### [145] [The small Davenport constant of $H_{27}\times C_3^r$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19810)
*Andreas Volkmann*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了27阶非交换群 H_{27}=UT_3(F_3) 与初等交换群 C_3^r 直积的小Davenport常数为 d(H_{27}×C_3^r)=2r+6，对任意 r≥0 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定有限非交换群直积的小Davenport常数是零和理论中的核心问题。此前结果往往依赖对小阶非交换群常数的已知值，或依赖计算机枚举，缺乏统一且自洽的证明方法。

Method: 证明结合了两个关键工具：初等交换群群代数中的仿射系数恒等式，以及将 F_3^2 的非正交图分解为八个边不相交的零和三角形。该方法对 r 一致，不依赖较小非交换群的小Davenport常数值，也不需要计算枚举。

Result: 证明了 d(H_{27}×C_3^r)=2r+6 对所有整数 r≥0 成立。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了该类非交换群直积小Davenport常数的显式闭式公式，并提供了不依赖计算枚举、对参数 r 一致的代数-组合证明范式。
